if (e != null) {
e.combatValue = combatValue;
e.touch();
}
}
public void updateEchelon(String id, String echelon) {
Entity e = entities.get(id);
if (e != null) {
e.echelon = echelon;
e.touch();
}
}
public void updateIsCommandPost(String id, boolean isPC) {
Entity e = entities.get(id);
if (e != null) {
e.isCommandPost = isPC;
e.touch();
}
}
public void updateUniqueId(String id, String uniqueId) {
Entity e = entities.get(id);
if (e != null) {
e.uniqueId = uniqueId;
e.touch();
}
}
public void remove(String id) {
entities.remove(id);
}
/**
* Drop all entity state. Used on reconnect: the entities were discovered in
* a federation this federate is no longer attached to, so keeping them
* would serve stale positions over the REST API until they happened to be
* rediscovered.
*/
public void clear() {
entities.clear();
simTime = 0.0;
}
public void setSimTime(double time) {
this.simTime = time;
}
public void setConnected(boolean connected) {
this.connected = connected;
}
public void setFederationName(String name) {
this.federationName = name;
}
/**
* DIS EntityIdentifier — the simulation's own name for this entity.
*
* <p>Stored as three fields plus a formatted "site-app-entity" string. The
* formatted form is what a REST caller addresses and what shows up in
* diagnostics; the numeric fields are what a SetData datum needs.
*/
public void updateEntityIdentifier(String id, int siteId, int applicationId, int entityNumber) {
Entity e = entities.get(id);
if (e != null) {
e.disSiteId         = siteId;
e.disApplicationId  = applicationId;
e.disEntityNumber   = entityNumber;
e.entityIdentifier  = siteId + "-" + applicationId + "-" + entityNumber;
e.touch();
}
}
// ── Read (called from REST API thread) ────────────────────────────────
public List<Entity> getAll() {
return new ArrayList<>(entities.values());
}
public Entity get(String id) {
return entities.get(id);
}
/**
* Find entity by UniqueId (NETN UUID) — needed for issuing interactions
* since ETR_Task uses UniqueId not HLA object handle.
*/
public Optional<Entity> getByUniqueId(String uniqueId) {
return entities.values().stream()
.filter(e -> uniqueId.equals(e.uniqueId))
.findFirst();
}
/**
* Find an entity by its DIS EntityIdentifier, written "site-app-entity".
*
* <p>The counterpart to {@link #getByUniqueId(String)} for simulators that
* publish no NETN UniqueId. Whitespace-tolerant, because this value is
* typed by hand far more often than a UUID is.
*/
public Optional<Entity> getByEntityIdentifier(String entityIdentifier) {
if (entityIdentifier == null) return Optional.empty();
String wanted = entityIdentifier.trim();
return entities.values().stream()
.filter(e -> wanted.equals(e.entityIdentifier))
.findFirst();
}
/** Same lookup, from the three numeric fields. */
public Optional<Entity> getByEntityIdentifier(int siteId, int applicationId, int entityNumber) {
return getByEntityIdentifier(siteId + "-" + applicationId + "-" + entityNumber);
}
/**
* Every distinct DIS site/application pair currently observed, with a count
* and an example entity.
*
* <p>This is how we learn MACE's and VBS4's real identifiers. A federate's
* site/app pair is effectively its address on the DIS side, so grouping the
* live entities by it says who is publishing what — which every later
* addressing step depends on, and which nothing else in the bridge reports.
*/
public List<Map<String, Object>> observedDisSourcesGrouped() {
Map<String, List<Entity>> bySource = new TreeMap<>();
for (Entity e : entities.values()) {
if (e.disSiteId == null || e.disApplicationId == null) continue;
bySource.computeIfAbsent(e.disSiteId + "-" + e.disApplicationId,
k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(e);
}
List<Map<String, Object>> out = new ArrayList<>();
bySource.forEach((source, group) -> {
Entity sample = group.get(0);
Map<String, Object> row = new LinkedHashMap<>();
row.put("siteApplication", source);
row.put("entityCount", group.size());
row.put("exampleEntityIdentifier", sample.entityIdentifier);
row.put("exampleName", sample.name);
row.put("exampleSimulator", sample.simulator);
row.put("exampleDiscoveredClass", sample.discoveredClass);
out.add(row);
});
return out;
}
/** Entities carrying a DIS EntityIdentifier, so addressable without a UniqueId. */
public List<Entity> getRprAddressable() {
return entities.values().stream()
.filter(e -> e.entityIdentifier != null)
.toList();
}
public List<Entity> getBySimulator(String simulator) {
return entities.values().stream()
.filter(e -> simulator.equalsIgnoreCase(e.simulator))
.toList();
}
public List<Entity> getInArea(double lat, double lon, double radiusKm) {
return entities.values().stream()
.filter(e -> e.latitude != null && e.longitude != null)
.filter(e -> haversineKm(lat, lon, e.latitude, e.longitude) <= radiusKm)
.toList();
}
public double getSimTime()       { return simTime; }
public boolean isConnected()     { return connected; }
public String getFederationName(){ return federationName; }
// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* Infer which simulator owns an entity from its DIS kind field.
* Kind=12 → Sword aggregate
* Kind=1  → Platform (VBS4 or SimWorks — distinguish by subcategory later)
* Kind=3  → Life Form (VBS4 dismounts)
*/
/**
* Which simulator published an entity, guessed from its object instance name.
*
* <p>Replaces a mapping from the DIS entity <em>kind</em>, which was simply
* wrong: per SISO-REF-010, kind 1 is Platform, 3 is Life form, 2 Munition,
* 4 Environmental, 5 Cultural feature — categories of thing, telling you nothing
* about which federate published it. Kind 12, previously mapped to "sword", is
* not in the enumeration at all. The result was confident, plausible and false:
* SWORD-published units were labelled "vbs4" because they were platforms.
*
* <p>The name prefix is a better guess because publishers put their own name
* there — SWORD registers instances as {@code SWORD_<id>_<unit name>}. It is
* still only a guess, and it is documented as one. The authoritative source
* would be the {@code producingFederate} argument to discoverObjectInstance,
* which the RTI supplies only when its conveyProducingFederate switch is on;
* it is off by default here.
*/
private String inferSimulator(String objectName) {
if (objectName == null || objectName.isBlank()) return "unknown";
int separator = objectName.indexOf('_');
String prefix = (separator > 0 ? objectName.substring(0, separator) : objectName)
.toLowerCase();
if (prefix.startsWith("sword"))    return "sword";
if (prefix.startsWith("vbs"))      return "vbs4";
if (prefix.startsWith("simworks")) return "simworks";
return "unknown";
}
private double haversineKm(double lat1, double lon1, double lat2, double lon2) {
double R = 6371.0;
double dLat = Math.toRadians(lat2 - lat1);
double dLon = Math.toRadians(lon2 - lon1);
double a = Math.sin(dLat/2) * Math.sin(dLat/2)
+ Math.cos(Math.toRadians(lat1)) * Math.cos(Math.toRadians(lat2))
* Math.sin(dLon/2) * Math.sin(dLon/2);
return R * 2 * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(a));
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/EnvironmentPorts.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
/**
* Resolves the FedPro/CRC addresses for a given CSE-Prep environment
* (namespace {@code cse-prep-simulation-0N}) from a single
* {@code CSE_ENVIRONMENT} number, per the port pattern
* {@code 30{ENVIRONMENT:1}{APPLICATION:2}}:
*
* <pre>
*   Environment N — CRC: 30N10, FedPro plain-TCP: 30N20, FedPro TLS: 30N21
* </pre>
*
* Every environment shares the same externally-exposed firewall host — only
* the port changes between environments.
*
* Explicit {@code FEDPRO_ADDRESS} / {@code CRC_ADDRESS} env vars always take
* priority over anything derived here — this is purely a convenience for
* switching environments with one value instead of computing exact ports by
* hand each time.
*/
final class EnvironmentPorts {
// Same firewall IP for every environment — only the NodePort differs.
private static final String FIREWALL_HOST = "4.165.4.53";
private EnvironmentPorts() {}
static String resolveFedproAddress(String cseEnvironment, String protocol, String explicitOverride) {
if (explicitOverride != null && !explicitOverride.isBlank()) {
return explicitOverride;
}
int env = parseEnvironmentNumber(cseEnvironment);
String appCode = "tls".equalsIgnoreCase(protocol) ? "21" : "20";
return FIREWALL_HOST + ":30" + env + appCode;
}
/**
* The literal candidate meaning "send no crcHost/crcPort at all", leaving the
* FedPro server to reach the CRC on whatever address it was configured with.
*/
static final String DEFAULT_CRC = "default";
/**
* The CRC addresses to try, in order, as a comma-separated list.
*
* <p>Why a list rather than one address: the CRC's address is not stable. With
* the Pitch <em>booster</em> enabled the CRC advertises a different host and
* port than when clients reach it directly, and a connect against the wrong
* one fails with
*
* <pre>Failed to connect to CRC at 'pitch-rti/172.30.170.11:8989'</pre>
*
* which is indistinguishable from the CRC being down. Trying each candidate
* and reporting which one answered turns that into a log line.
*
* <p>Order matters. {@link #DEFAULT_CRC} is first because it is what works
* when the CRC is directly reachable — it has connected on this deployment
* repeatedly — and only when that fails is it worth naming an address
* ourselves. {@code CRC_ADDRESS} entries, if given, are tried before all of
* them; the derived candidates are always appended so an explicit value can
* never leave the bridge with no fallback.
*/
static String resolveCrcAddress(String cseEnvironment, String explicitOverride) {
int env = parseEnvironmentNumber(cseEnvironment);
java.util.LinkedHashSet<String> candidates = new java.util.LinkedHashSet<>();
if (explicitOverride != null && !explicitOverride.isBlank()) {
candidates.addAll(splitCandidates(explicitOverride));
}
candidates.add(DEFAULT_CRC);
// Booster mode: the CRC's advertised host changes to the booster's, and
// the booster listens on 8688 rather than the CRC's 8989.
candidates.add(String.format(
"pitch-booster.cse-prep-simulation-%02d.svc.cluster.local:8688", env));
// The CRC's own in-cluster service address, fully qualified and short.
candidates.add(String.format(
"pitch-rti.cse-prep-simulation-%02d.svc.cluster.local:8989", env));
candidates.add("pitch-rti:8989");
return String.join(",", candidates);
}
/** Split a comma-separated candidate list, trimming blanks. */
static java.util.List<String> splitCandidates(String value) {
java.util.List<String> out = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
if (value == null) {
return out;
}
for (String part : value.split(",")) {
String trimmed = part.trim();
if (!trimmed.isEmpty() && !out.contains(trimmed)) {
out.add(trimmed);
}
}
return out;
}
static int parseEnvironmentNumber(String cseEnvironment) {
try {
int n = Integer.parseInt(cseEnvironment.trim());
if (n < 1 || n > 9) {
throw new NumberFormatException("out of range: " + n);
}
return n;
} catch (Exception e) {
// Fail loudly rather than silently falling back to environment 1 —
// a broken CSE_ENVIRONMENT value should be obvious at startup.
throw new IllegalArgumentException(
"CSE_ENVIRONMENT must be a single digit 1-9 (per the 30{ENVIRONMENT}{APPLICATION}"
+ " port pattern) — got: '" + cseEnvironment + "'",
e);
}
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/EtrTaskSender.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import com.decho.cse.bridge.BridgeFederate.Fom;
import hla.rti1516e.InteractionClassHandle;
import hla.rti1516e.ParameterHandleValueMap;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
/**
* Sends NETN tasks and RPR simulation-management interactions.
*
* <h2>What this class does now</h2>
* Tasking itself lives in {@link TaskDialect}: NETN v2-era and NETN v4 model it
* so differently — different class tree, different parameter names, different
* datatypes, different units — that one implementation cannot serve both, and
* the bridge does not get to choose which the federation speaks. This class is
* the seam: it delegates each REST operation to whichever dialect
* {@link BridgeFederate} selected from the FOM actually loaded, and keeps the
* two things that are genuinely dialect independent.
*
* <p>Those two are scenario start/pause/stop, which is not an ETR concept at all
* — it uses the DIS-derived RPR-FOM {@code StartResume} / {@code StopFreeze}
* interactions — and the DIS site/application identity they are sent with.
*
* @see TaskDialect for the v2/v4 differences
* @see EtrV2Dialect
* @see SmcV4Dialect
*/
@Component
public class EtrTaskSender {
private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(EtrTaskSender.class);
private final BridgeFederate federate;
private final BridgeDiagnostics diagnostics;
private volatile HlaEncoder encoder;
private final int disSiteId;
private final int disApplicationId;
public EtrTaskSender(BridgeFederate federate,
BridgeDiagnostics diagnostics,
@Value("${bridge.dis-site-id}") int disSiteId,
@Value("${bridge.dis-application-id}") int disApplicationId) {
this.federate         = federate;
this.diagnostics      = diagnostics;
this.disSiteId        = disSiteId;
this.disApplicationId = disApplicationId;
}
/**
* Send a task and record it, so that "what did we send, and did anything
* answer?" can be asked over HTTP later.
*
* <p>Wrapping every delegation in one place is the reason this facade earns
* its keep: the record is written exactly once per task, with the operation,
* the FOM class the live dialect chose for it, and the entity — the three
* things needed to correlate a task with an acknowledgement, or with the
* simulator's own logs.
*/
private UUID sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op op, String entityUuid, TaskSend send)
throws Exception {
TaskDialect dialect = federate.dialect();
UUID taskId = send.execute(dialect);
diagnostics.recordTaskSent(taskId.toString(), op.name(), dialect.classFor(op),
dialect.id(), entityUuid);
return taskId;
}
/** The dialect call to make. Separate so the recording cannot be forgotten. */
private interface TaskSend {
UUID execute(TaskDialect dialect) throws Exception;
}
/**
* The RTI encoder factory, created on first use. Doing this lazily rather
* than in the constructor keeps a classpath/RTI problem from failing Spring
* startup, which would take the /health and /status endpoints down with it
* and hide the actual error.
*/
private HlaEncoder enc() throws Exception {
HlaEncoder local = encoder;
if (local == null) {
synchronized (this) {
local = encoder;
if (local == null) {
local = new HlaEncoder();
encoder = local;
}
}
}
return local;
}
/** The dialect in use, for /status and for logging what a task was sent as. */
public String dialectName() {
return federate.dialectName();
}
public String dialectId() {
return federate.dialectId();
}
// ── Tasking (delegated to the live dialect) ───────────────────────────────
public UUID moveToLocation(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond,
String annotation) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.MOVE, entityUuid, dialect -> dialect.moveToLocation(
entityUuid, latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters, moveType, speedMetersPerSecond,
annotation));
}
public UUID patrol(String entityUuid,
List<double[]> waypointsLatLonAlt,
String patrolType,
String patrolMoveType,
Integer durationSeconds,
Integer intervalSeconds,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.PATROL, entityUuid, dialect -> dialect.patrol(
entityUuid, waypointsLatLonAlt, patrolType, patrolMoveType,
durationSeconds, intervalSeconds, speedMetersPerSecond));
}
public UUID magicMove(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
double headingDegrees) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.MAGIC_MOVE, entityUuid, dialect -> dialect.magicMove(
entityUuid, latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters, headingDegrees));
}
public UUID fireAtLocation(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
int durationSeconds) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.FIRE_AT_LOCATION, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.fireAtLocation(
entityUuid, latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters, durationSeconds));
}
public UUID fireAtEntity(String entityUuid, String targetUuid) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.FIRE_AT_ENTITY, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.fireAtEntity(entityUuid, targetUuid));
}
public UUID setRulesOfEngagement(String entityUuid, String roe) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.ROE, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.setRulesOfEngagement(entityUuid, roe));
}
public UUID changeHeading(String entityUuid, double headingDegrees) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.CHANGE_HEADING, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.changeHeading(entityUuid, headingDegrees));
}
public UUID changeSpeed(String entityUuid, double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.CHANGE_SPEED, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.changeSpeed(entityUuid, speedMetersPerSecond));
}
public UUID changeAltitude(String entityUuid, double altitudeMeters) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.CHANGE_ALTITUDE, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.changeAltitude(entityUuid, altitudeMeters));
}
public UUID stopAtSideOfRoad(String entityUuid) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.STOP, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.stopAtSideOfRoad(entityUuid));
}
public UUID cancelAllTasks(String entityUuid) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.CANCEL_TASKS, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.cancelAllTasks(entityUuid));
}
public UUID injectSupplies(String entityUuid, String resourceType, String supplyType,
double quantity) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.INJECT_SUPPLIES, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.injectSupplies(entityUuid, resourceType, supplyType, quantity));
}
public UUID createObstacle(String entityUuid,
String obstacleName,
String obstacleType,
double latitude,
double longitude,

return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.CREATE_OBSTACLE, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.createObstacle(entityUuid, obstacleName, obstacleType,
latitude, longitude, radiusMeters));
}
public UUID disruptCommunication(String entityUuid,
String network,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double radiusKm,
boolean enabled) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(TaskDialect.Op.DISRUPT_COMMS, entityUuid,
dialect -> dialect.disruptCommunication(
entityUuid, network, latitude, longitude, radiusKm, enabled));
}
public UUID multiResolution(boolean aggregate,
String aggregateUuid,
String federateName,
boolean removeSubunits) throws Exception {
return sendAndRecord(
aggregate ? TaskDialect.Op.AGGREGATE : TaskDialect.Op.DISAGGREGATE, aggregateUuid,
dialect -> dialect.multiResolution(
aggregate, aggregateUuid, federateName, removeSubunits));
}
/** The FOM class the live dialect would send for an operation, for /diagnostics. */
public String classFor(TaskDialect.Op op) {
return federate.dialect().classFor(op);
}
/**
* What a move would put on the wire, without sending it.
*
* <p>For handing to another federate's owner when a task is accepted and
* nothing happens: it shows the field values, the datatypes and the bytes, so
* an ECEF-versus-degrees disagreement is visible rather than inferred.
*/
public java.util.Map<String, String> describeMoveTask(double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond)
throws Exception {
return federate.dialect().describeMoveTask(
latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters, moveType, speedMetersPerSecond);
}
// ── Scenario control (RPR simulation management) ──────────────────────────
// Dialect independent: NETN has no "start/pause/stop scenario" interaction
// in either version. These are the DIS-derived RPR-FOM equivalents, and they
// are also the four classes that worked before any of the NETN tasking did.
/** {@code StartResume} — broadcast to the whole federation. */
public long startResume() throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = federate.interaction(Fom.START_RESUME);
long requestId = nextRequestId();
Instant now = Instant.now();
ParameterHandleValueMap params = federate.newParameterMap(5);
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "OriginatingEntity"),
enc().entityIdentifier(disSiteId, disApplicationId, 0));
// EntityNumber 0 with the broadcast site/application means "all".
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "ReceivingEntity"), enc().entityIdentifier(0, 0, 0));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "RequestIdentifier"), enc().int32((int) requestId));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "RealWorldTime"), enc().clockTime(now));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "SimulationTime"), enc().clockTime(now));
federate.sendInteraction(handle, params);
logger.info("StartResume sent (request {}).", requestId);
return requestId;
}
/**
* {@code StopFreeze}.
*
* @param pause true for a pause (Reason=Recess, internal clock keeps
*              running), false for a full stop (Reason=Termination)
*/
public long stopFreeze(boolean pause) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = federate.interaction(Fom.STOP_FREEZE);
long requestId = nextRequestId();
ParameterHandleValueMap params = federate.newParameterMap(8);
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "OriginatingEntity"),
enc().entityIdentifier(disSiteId, disApplicationId, 0));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "ReceivingEntity"), enc().entityIdentifier(0, 0, 0));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "RequestIdentifier"), enc().int32((int) requestId));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "RealWorldTime"), enc().clockTime(Instant.now()));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "Reason"), enc().octet(
pause ? TaskEnums.STOP_REASON_RECESS : TaskEnums.STOP_REASON_TERMINATION));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "ReflectValues"), enc().rprBoolean(true));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "RunInternalSimulationClock"),
enc().rprBoolean(pause));
params.put(federate.parameter(handle, "UpdateAttributes"), enc().rprBoolean(pause));
federate.sendInteraction(handle, params);
logger.info("StopFreeze sent (request {}, reason {}).",
requestId, pause ? "Recess/pause" : "Termination/stop");
return requestId;
}
private static int requestCounter = 0;
private static synchronized long nextRequestId() {
return ++requestCounter;
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/EtrV2Dialect.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import com.decho.cse.bridge.BridgeFederate.Fom;
import hla.rti1516e.InteractionClassHandle;
import hla.rti1516e.ParameterHandleValueMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
/**
* NETN v2-era tasking — {@code ETR_Root.ETR_Task.*} and siblings.
*
* <h2>The model</h2>
* <ul>
*   <li>{@code ETR_Task} is abstract (sharing=Neither) — the RTI will not let
*       any federate publish or send it. Only its concrete subclasses can be
*       sent, and <em>the subclass is what identifies the task</em>. There is no
*       {@code TaskType} parameter in any NETN FOM.</li>
*   <li>Every task carries the inherited parameters {@code TaskId},
*       {@code Taskee}, {@code Tasker}, {@code StartWhen}, {@code Why},
*       {@code TaskMode} plus a parameter set specific to that subclass.</li>
*   <li>{@code Taskee} is a NETN {@code UniqueId} (a 16-byte UUID), not an HLA
*       object-instance handle and not a marking string.</li>
* </ul>
*
* <p>{@code ETR_SimCon} ("magic"/umpire actions such as MagicMove and
* MagicResource) and {@code ETR_TaskManagement} (CancelAllTasks,
* TaskStatusReport) are sibling branches with their own, slightly different,
* base parameters — SimCon has no {@code StartWhen}/{@code TaskMode}, and
* TaskManagement names its identifier {@code TaskManagementId} rather than
* {@code TaskId}.
*
* <p>This is the dialect {@code cse-hla-bridge/foms/SWORD_FOM.xml} defines, so
* it stays supported: a federation created by SWORD will speak it, and removing
* it to make a v4 federation work would break the case the bridge was
* originally built for.
*/
class EtrV2Dialect extends BaseDialect {
EtrV2Dialect(BridgeFederate federate, Identity identity) {
super(federate, identity);
}
@Override public String id()   { return "v2"; }
@Override public String name() { return "NETN v2-era (ETR_Root.ETR_Task)"; }
@Override public String detectionClass() { return Fom.MOVE_TO_LOCATION; }
@Override
public String classFor(Op op) {
return switch (op) {
case MOVE              -> Fom.MOVE_TO_LOCATION;
// Patrol picks its class per request: a duration means the
// PatrolRepeating subclass. Report the base class here.
case PATROL            -> Fom.PATROL;
case MAGIC_MOVE        -> Fom.MAGIC_MOVE;
case FIRE_AT_LOCATION  -> Fom.FIRE_AT_LOCATION;
case FIRE_AT_ENTITY    -> Fom.FIRE_AT_ENTITY;
case ROE               -> Fom.SET_RULES_OF_ENGAGEMENT;
case CANCEL_TASKS      -> Fom.CANCEL_ALL_TASKS;
case INJECT_SUPPLIES   -> Fom.MAGIC_RESOURCE;
case CREATE_OBSTACLE   -> Fom.CREATE_OBSTACLE;
case DISRUPT_COMMS     -> Fom.DISRUPT_COMMUNICATION;
case AGGREGATE         -> Fom.MRM_AGGREGATE;
case DISAGGREGATE      -> Fom.MRM_DISAGGREGATE;
case CHANGE_HEADING    -> Fom.TURN_TO_HEADING;
case CHANGE_SPEED      -> Fom.SET_ORDERED_SPEED;
case CHANGE_ALTITUDE   -> Fom.SET_ORDERED_ALTITUDE;
case STOP              -> Fom.STOP_AT_SIDE_OF_ROAD;
};
}
// ── Simple scalar tasks ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// v2 names these differently from v4 and, for heading, uses different units.
// Parameter names and datatypes verified against foms/SWORD_FOM.xml.
@Override
public UUID changeHeading(String entityUuid, double headingDegrees) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(Fom.TURN_TO_HEADING);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge heading change", true);
// AngleRadianFloat32 — v2 wants radians where v4 wants degrees, and the
// REST API speaks degrees. Sending degrees here would be a plausible
// 0-360 value interpreted as up to 57 full turns.
put(params, handle, "Heading",
enc().float32(Math.toRadians(TaskEnums.normaliseDegrees(headingDegrees))));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("TurnToHeading task {} -> {} ({} deg true, sent as radians)",
taskId, entityUuid, headingDegrees);
return taskId;
}
@Override
public UUID changeSpeed(String entityUuid, double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(Fom.SET_ORDERED_SPEED);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge speed change", true);
put(params, handle, "Speed", enc().float32(speedMetersPerSecond));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("SetOrderedSpeed task {} -> {} ({} m/s)", taskId, entityUuid,
speedMetersPerSecond);
return taskId;
}
@Override
public UUID changeAltitude(String entityUuid, double altitudeMeters) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(Fom.SET_ORDERED_ALTITUDE);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge altitude change", true);
put(params, handle, "Altitude", enc().float64(altitudeMeters));
// AltitudeType (AltitudeTypeEnum8) is deliberately omitted: its
// enumerators are not defined in the module we hold, and guessing one
// would silently pick a reference datum. Omitted means the receiver's
// default applies, which is the honest behaviour.
send(handle, params);
logger.info("SetOrderedAltitude task {} -> {} ({} m, AltitudeType omitted)",
taskId, entityUuid, altitudeMeters);
return taskId;
}
@Override
public UUID stopAtSideOfRoad(String entityUuid) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(Fom.STOP_AT_SIDE_OF_ROAD);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge stop order", false);
send(handle, params);
logger.info("StopAtSideOfRoad task {} -> {}", taskId, entityUuid);
return taskId;
}
@Override
public List<String> published() {
return List.of(
Fom.MOVE_TO_LOCATION,
Fom.PATROL,
Fom.PATROL_REPEATING,
Fom.FIRE_AT_LOCATION,
Fom.FIRE_AT_ENTITY,
Fom.SET_RULES_OF_ENGAGEMENT,
Fom.CREATE_OBSTACLE,
Fom.DISRUPT_COMMUNICATION,
Fom.MAGIC_MOVE,
Fom.MAGIC_RESOURCE,
Fom.CANCEL_ALL_TASKS,
Fom.TURN_TO_HEADING,
Fom.SET_ORDERED_SPEED,
Fom.SET_ORDERED_ALTITUDE,
Fom.STOP_AT_SIDE_OF_ROAD,
Fom.MRM_AGGREGATE,
Fom.MRM_DISAGGREGATE);
}
@Override
public List<String> subscribed() {
return List.of(
Fom.TASK_STATUS_REPORT,
Fom.CAPABILITIES_SUPPORTED,
Fom.POSITION_STATUS_REPORT,
Fom.DAMAGE_STATUS_REPORT,
Fom.RESOURCE_STATUS_REPORT,
Fom.UNDER_ATTACK_REPORT,
Fom.SPOT_REPORT,
Fom.IN_WEAPON_RANGE_REPORT,
Fom.MRM_RESPONSE);
}
@Override
public List<String> optional() {
// Present in the FOM and usable, but no REST endpoint sends them yet.
// Resolved so the startup log records whether this federation offers
// them at all.
return List.of(
Fom.MOVE_TO_ENTITY,
Fom.CLEAR_OBSTACLE,
Fom.QUERY_CAPABILITIES,
Fom.CANCEL_SPECIFIED_TASKS);
}
// ── Base parameter builders ───────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* Parameters inherited by every {@code ETR_Task} subclass.
*
* @param taskeeUuid NETN UniqueId of the entity to task
* @param why        free text recorded with the task (optional)
*/
private ParameterHandleValueMap taskBase(InteractionClassHandle handle,
UUID taskId,
String taskeeUuid,
String why,
boolean concurrent) throws Exception {
ParameterHandleValueMap params = newParams(8);
put(params, handle, "TaskId", enc().uuid(taskId));
put(params, handle, "Taskee", enc().uuid(taskeeUuid));
put(params, handle, "Tasker", enc().unicodeString(identity.taskerCallsign()));
// Datetime18 all-zero = start immediately.
put(params, handle, "StartWhen", enc().datetime18(null));
put(params, handle, "TaskMode", enc().octet(
concurrent ? TaskEnums.TASK_MODE_CONCURRENT : TaskEnums.TASK_MODE_NON_CONCURRENT));
if (why != null && !why.isBlank()) {
putOptional(params, handle, "Why", enc().unicodeString(why));
}
return params;
}
/** Parameters inherited by every {@code ETR_SimCon} subclass. */
private ParameterHandleValueMap simConBase(InteractionClassHandle handle,
UUID taskId,
String taskeeUuid) throws Exception {
ParameterHandleValueMap params = newParams(4);
put(params, handle, "TaskId", enc().uuid(taskId));
put(params, handle, "Taskee", enc().uuid(taskeeUuid));
put(params, handle, "Tasker", enc().unicodeString(identity.taskerCallsign()));
return params;
}
/** Parameters inherited by every {@code ETR_TaskManagement} subclass. */
private ParameterHandleValueMap taskManagementBase(InteractionClassHandle handle,
UUID managementId,
String taskeeUuid) throws Exception {
ParameterHandleValueMap params = newParams(4);
put(params, handle, "TaskManagementId", enc().uuid(managementId));
put(params, handle, "Taskee", enc().uuid(taskeeUuid));
put(params, handle, "Tasker", enc().unicodeString(identity.taskerCallsign()));
return params;
}
// ── Movement ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** {@code ETR_Task.MoveToLocation}. */
@Override
public UUID moveToLocation(String taskeeUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond,
String annotation) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(Fom.MOVE_TO_LOCATION);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params = taskBase(handle, taskId, taskeeUuid,
annotation == null || annotation.isBlank() ? "CSE bridge move order" : annotation,
false);
put(params, handle, "Location", enc().worldLocation(latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters));
put(params, handle, "MoveType", enc().int32(TaskEnums.moveType(moveType)));
if (speedMetersPerSecond != null) {
// v2 MoveToLocation has no Speed parameter; SetOrderedSpeed is a
// separate task. Say so rather than dropping the value silently.
logger.info("Ignoring speedMetersPerSecond={} — the v2 MoveToLocation task has no "
+ "Speed parameter. Send SetOrderedSpeed separately.", speedMetersPerSecond);
}
send(handle, params);
logger.info("MoveToLocation task {} -> {} ({}, {})", taskId, taskeeUuid, latitude, longitude);
return taskId;
}
@Override
public Map<String, String> describeMoveTask(double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
// v2 has no TaskParameters struct: Location and MoveType are parameters of
// the interaction itself, and there is no Speed field at all.
double[] ecef = HlaEncoder.wgs84ToEcef(latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters);
Map<String, String> fields = new LinkedHashMap<>();
fields.put("interactionClass", Fom.MOVE_TO_LOCATION);
fields.put("Location.datatype",
"WorldLocationStruct = 3 x HLAfloat64BE {X, Y, Z}, geocentric ECEF metres");
fields.put("Location.input",
String.format("latitude %.6f, longitude %.6f, altitude %.1f m (WGS-84)",
latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters));
fields.put("Location.encodedAs",
String.format("X=%.3f Y=%.3f Z=%.3f (metres)", ecef[0], ecef[1], ecef[2]));
fields.put("Location.hex",
HlaEncoder.hex(enc().worldLocation(latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters)));
fields.put("MoveType",
"MoveTypeEnum32 = " + TaskEnums.moveType(moveType) + " from moveType='"
+ moveType + "'");
fields.put("Speed", "not present in the v2 MoveToLocation task"
+ (speedMetersPerSecond == null ? "" : " — the supplied "
+ speedMetersPerSecond + " m/s would be ignored"));
fields.put("inheritedParameters",
"TaskId (UUID), Taskee (UUID), Tasker (Callsign string '"
+ identity.taskerCallsign() + "'), StartWhen (Datetime18, all zeroes = "
+ "immediately), TaskMode (octet), Why (HLAunicodeString)");
return fields;
}
/** {@code ETR_Task.Patrol}, or {@code Patrol.PatrolRepeating} when a duration is given. */
@Override
public UUID patrol(String taskeeUuid,
List<double[]> waypointsLatLonAlt,
String patrolType,
String patrolMoveType,
Integer durationSeconds,
Integer intervalSeconds,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
boolean repeating = durationSeconds != null && durationSeconds > 0;
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(repeating ? Fom.PATROL_REPEATING : Fom.PATROL);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, taskeeUuid, "CSE bridge patrol order", false);
put(params, handle, "Path", enc().arrayOfWorldLocation(waypointsLatLonAlt));
put(params, handle, "PatrolType", enc().int32(TaskEnums.patrolType(patrolType)));
put(params, handle, "MoveType", enc().int32(TaskEnums.patrolMoveType(patrolMoveType)));
if (repeating) {
// v2 durations are seconds as int32.
put(params, handle, "Duration", enc().int32(durationSeconds));
put(params, handle, "IntervalTime",
enc().int32(intervalSeconds == null ? 0 : intervalSeconds));
}
if (speedMetersPerSecond != null) {
logger.info("Ignoring speedMetersPerSecond={} — the v2 Patrol path is an array of "
+ "locations with no per-waypoint speed.", speedMetersPerSecond);
}
send(handle, params);
logger.info("{} task {} -> {} ({} waypoints)",
repeating ? "PatrolRepeating" : "Patrol", taskId, taskeeUuid,
waypointsLatLonAlt.size());
return taskId;
}

}

// ── Environment ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* {@code ETR_Task.CreateObstacle}. Note this is a task given to an engineer
* entity — the FOM requires a {@code Taskee}; there is no way to create an
* obstacle "from nowhere".
*/
@Override
public UUID createObstacle(String taskeeUuid,
String obstacleName,
String obstacleType,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double radiusMeters) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(Fom.CREATE_OBSTACLE);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
UUID obstacleUuid = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, taskeeUuid, "CSE bridge obstacle: " + obstacleName, false);
put(params, handle, "UniqueId", enc().uuid(obstacleUuid));
put(params, handle, "ObstacleType",
enc().entityType(TaskEnums.obstacleType(obstacleName, obstacleType)));
put(params, handle, "Geometry", enc().areaCircle(latitude, longitude, radiusMeters));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("CreateObstacle task {} -> {} obstacle {} ({}, {}) r={}m",
taskId, taskeeUuid, obstacleUuid, latitude, longitude, radiusMeters);
return obstacleUuid;
}
/**
* {@code ETR_Task.DisruptCommunication}. Also a task on an entity (the EW
* asset doing the jamming), over a geodetic quadrangle rather than a radius.
*/
@Override
public UUID disruptCommunication(String taskeeUuid,
String network,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double radiusKm,
boolean enabled) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(Fom.DISRUPT_COMMUNICATION);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, taskeeUuid, "CSE bridge comms disruption", true);
put(params, handle, "AffectedCommunicationNetwork",
enc().unicodeString(network == null ? "" : network));
// AffectedArea is a GeodeticQuadrangle (two corner points), so the
// requested radius is converted into a bounding box around the centre.
double dLat = radiusKm / 111.32;
double dLon = radiusKm / (111.32 * Math.max(0.01, Math.cos(Math.toRadians(latitude))));
put(params, handle, "AffectedArea",
enc().geodeticQuadrangle(latitude - dLat, longitude - dLon,
latitude + dLat, longitude + dLon));
put(params, handle, "DisruptionEnabled", enc().hlaBoolean(enabled));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("DisruptCommunication task {} -> {} {} around ({}, {}) r={}km",
taskId, taskeeUuid, enabled ? "enabled" : "disabled", latitude, longitude, radiusKm);
return taskId;
}
// ── Multi-resolution modelling ────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* {@code MRM_Interaction.Request.Aggregate} / {@code .Disaggregate}.
*
* <p>Unlike ETR tasks these are addressed to a <em>federate</em> (the one
* with modelling responsibility for the aggregate), and the unit is
* identified by the {@code AggregateUnit} UUID of a NETN_Aggregate object.
*/
@Override
public UUID multiResolution(boolean aggregate,
String aggregateUnitUuid,
String federateName,
boolean removeSubunits) throws Exception {
String target = (federateName == null || federateName.isBlank())
? identity.defaultMrmFederate() : federateName;
if (target == null || target.isBlank()) {
throw new IllegalArgumentException(
"MRM aggregate/disaggregate requires the name of the federate responsible "
+ "for the unit. Pass \"federate\" in the request body or set MRM_FEDERATE.");
}
InteractionClassHandle handle =
interaction(aggregate ? Fom.MRM_AGGREGATE : Fom.MRM_DISAGGREGATE);
UUID eventId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params = newParams(4);
put(params, handle, "EventId", enc().uuid(eventId));
put(params, handle, "Federate", enc().unicodeString(target));
put(params, handle, "AggregateUnit", enc().uuid(aggregateUnitUuid));
if (aggregate) {
put(params, handle, "RemoveSubunits", enc().hlaBoolean(removeSubunits));
}
send(handle, params);
logger.info("MRM {} event {} unit {} federate {}",
aggregate ? "Aggregate" : "Disaggregate", eventId, aggregateUnitUuid, target);
return eventId;
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/FederationAmbassador.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import hla.rti1516e.*;
import hla.rti1516e.encoding.*;
import hla.rti1516e.exceptions.*;
import hla.rti1516e.time.HLAfloat64Time;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
public class FederationAmbassador extends NullFederateAmbassador {
private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(FederationAmbassador.class);
private final EntityStateStore store;
private final RTIambassador rtiAmbassador;
// Concurrent, not plain HashMaps. These are written by the connect thread while
// it sets up pub/sub, and read by RTI callback threads delivering discovery and
// reflection at the same moment — HLA_IMMEDIATE means those callbacks start
// arriving while subscription is still in progress. A HashMap resized during a
// concurrent read can return a stale miss or, worse, corrupt its own table.
private final Map<AttributeHandle, String>          attrNames  =
new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
private final Map<InteractionClassHandle, String>   interNames =
new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
private final Map<ParameterHandle, String>          paramNames =
new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
private final Map<ObjectClassHandle, String>        classNames =
new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
/** Object instances that are MOM federate records rather than entities. */
private final java.util.Set<String> momInstances =
java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<>());
/** Invoked when the RTI reports the connection lost; may be null. */
private final Runnable connectionLostHandler;
/** Observational state for /diagnostics. Never affects what is decoded. */
private final BridgeDiagnostics diagnostics;
public FederationAmbassador(RTIambassador rtiAmbassador, EntityStateStore store) {
this(rtiAmbassador, store, new BridgeDiagnostics(), null);
}
public FederationAmbassador(RTIambassador rtiAmbassador,
EntityStateStore store,
BridgeDiagnostics diagnostics,
Runnable connectionLostHandler) {
this.rtiAmbassador         = rtiAmbassador;
this.store                 = store;
this.diagnostics           = diagnostics;
this.connectionLostHandler = connectionLostHandler;
}
public void registerAttributeName(AttributeHandle h, String name)          { attrNames.put(h, name); }
public void registerInteractionName(InteractionClassHandle h, String name) { interNames.put(h, name); }
public void registerParameterName(ParameterHandle h, String name)          { paramNames.put(h, name); }
public void registerObjectClassName(ObjectClassHandle h, String name)      { classNames.put(h, name); }
// ── Asking for current values ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//
// Subscribing only entitles this federate to attribute updates the publisher
// chooses to send *from now on*. HLA publishers typically send on change, so a
// federate that joins an already-running federation — which this bridge always
// does — sees discoverObjectInstance for every entity and then nothing until
// something moves. The symptom is an entity list full of names with
// lastUpdated == discoveredAt, no position and entityType "unknown", which
// reads as a decoding bug and is not one: nothing has been sent to decode.
//
// requestAttributeValueUpdate asks the owner to publish current values now.
// It is the standard remedy for a late joiner, and it is also what makes a
// paused scenario visible rather than empty.
/**
* The attributes subscribed per object class, for value requests.
*
* <p>Concurrent for the same reason as the name maps above: populated by the
* connect thread, read by callback threads during the discovery burst that
* subscribing triggers. A miss here is not harmless — it makes
* {@link #requestCurrentValues} ask for nothing, and the entity stays a name
* with no state.
*/
private final Map<ObjectClassHandle, AttributeHandleSet> subscribedAttributes =
new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
/** Discovered instances and their class, so values can be re-requested later. */
private final Map<ObjectInstanceHandle, ObjectClassHandle> discoveredClasses =
new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
/**
* The subscribed attributes that have actually arrived for each instance.
*
* <p>This is what lets the periodic refresh stop asking. Re-requesting every
* attribute of every instance on every cycle is not free: the FedPro client
* runs with {@code asyncUpdates=false} by default, documented as "ignore error
* responses for sent HLA updates, allowing non-blocking HLA update calls" —
* so with it off, every call is a blocking round trip to the CRC. Measured on
* CSE-Prep, one such round trip costs 0.7s to 8.5s. At 116 discovered objects
* that is 80+ seconds of blocking per cycle, against a cycle time of five
* seconds, and the LRC's outbound queue (default {@code messageQueue.size} of
* 2000) never drains. A federate whose LRC is saturated stops answering the
* CRC's heartbeat, and the CRC ejects it — which is what
* "Ejecting non-responsive federate" in the CRC log means.
*
* <p>An instance that has reported everything we subscribed to needs nothing
* more, so in the steady state this drops the refresh to zero calls.
*/
private final Map<ObjectInstanceHandle, java.util.Set<AttributeHandle>> receivedAttributes =
new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
/** Rotates the starting point so a capped cycle cannot starve the same tail forever. */
private int refreshCursor = 0;
public void registerSubscribedAttributes(ObjectClassHandle h, AttributeHandleSet attributes) {
subscribedAttributes.put(h, attributes);
}
/**
* Ask the owning federate for the current value of everything we subscribe to
* on this instance.
*
* <p>Best-effort by design: the request can be refused, ignored, or arrive
* before the publisher is ready, and none of that is worth failing a discovery
* callback over. {@link #requestValuesForAllInstances()} covers the retry.
*/
private void requestCurrentValues(ObjectInstanceHandle instance, ObjectClassHandle objectClass) {
AttributeHandleSet attributes = outstandingAttributes(instance, objectClass);
if (attributes == null) {
return;
}
try {
rtiAmbassador.requestAttributeValueUpdate(instance, attributes, null);
} catch (Exception e) {
log.debug("Could not request attribute values for {}: {}", instance, e.getMessage());
}
}
/**
* The subscribed attributes this instance has not reported yet, or null if it
* has reported all of them (or we subscribe to none of them).
*
* <p>Returning the full set for an instance we have heard nothing from is
* deliberate: that is the late-joiner case the refresh exists for.
*/
private AttributeHandleSet outstandingAttributes(ObjectInstanceHandle instance,
ObjectClassHandle objectClass) {
AttributeHandleSet subscribed = subscribedAttributes.get(objectClass);
if (subscribed == null || subscribed.isEmpty()) {
return null;
}
java.util.Set<AttributeHandle> arrived = receivedAttributes.get(instance);
if (arrived == null) {
return subscribed;
}
if (arrived.containsAll(subscribed)) {
return null;
}
try {
AttributeHandleSet missing = rtiAmbassador.getAttributeHandleSetFactory().create();
for (AttributeHandle handle : subscribed) {
if (!arrived.contains(handle)) {
missing.add(handle);
}
}
return missing.isEmpty() ? null : missing;
} catch (Exception e) {
// Could not build a narrowed set; asking for the full one is correct,
// just less economical.
return subscribed;
}
}
/** Summary of one refresh pass, for the run loop's log line. */
public record RefreshSummary(int asked, int outstanding, int complete, boolean capped) {}
/**
* Re-request current values for every discovered instance.
*
* <p>Called periodically from the run loop. One request on discovery is not
* always enough: the publisher may not have been ready, the scenario may have
* been loaded after we joined, or the response may simply have been dropped.
*
* @return how many instances were asked
*/
public RefreshSummary requestValuesForAllInstances(int maxRequests) {
List<Map.Entry<ObjectInstanceHandle, ObjectClassHandle>> incomplete = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
int complete = 0;
for (Map.Entry<ObjectInstanceHandle, ObjectClassHandle> entry : discoveredClasses.entrySet()) {
if (outstandingAttributes(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue()) == null) {
complete++;
} else {
incomplete.add(entry);
}
}
if (incomplete.isEmpty()) {
return new RefreshSummary(0, 0, complete, false);
}
// Start where the last pass stopped. Without this a cap smaller than the
// backlog would re-ask the same head every cycle and never reach the tail.
int limit = maxRequests > 0 ? Math.min(maxRequests, incomplete.size()) : incomplete.size();
int start = Math.floorMod(refreshCursor, incomplete.size());
for (int i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
Map.Entry<ObjectInstanceHandle, ObjectClassHandle> entry =
incomplete.get((start + i) % incomplete.size());
requestCurrentValues(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue());
}
refreshCursor = start + limit;
return new RefreshSummary(limit, incomplete.size(), complete, limit < incomplete.size());
}
// No explicit clear for discoveredClasses: a new FederationAmbassador is built
// for every connect attempt, so stale instance handles go with the old one.
// ── Object lifecycle ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// BOTH discoverObjectInstance overloads must be implemented. FedPro's
// FederateAmbassadorClientAdapter picks between them at runtime:
//
//     if (producingFederate != null) -> discoverObjectInstance(4 args)
//     else                           -> discoverObjectInstance(3 args)
//
// producingFederate is only supplied when the RTI's conveyProducingFederate
// switch is on, and that switch is off by default. Overriding only the
// 4-arg version (as before) means discovery quietly falls through to
// NullFederateAmbassador's no-op and no entity is ever seen.
@Override
public void discoverObjectInstance(
ObjectInstanceHandle theObject,
ObjectClassHandle    theObjectClass,
String               objectName)
throws FederateInternalError {
discoverObjectInstance(theObject, theObjectClass, objectName, null);
}
@Override
public void discoverObjectInstance(
ObjectInstanceHandle theObject,
ObjectClassHandle    theObjectClass,
String               objectName,
FederateHandle       producingFederate)
throws FederateInternalError {
String id         = theObject.toString();
String className  = classNames.getOrDefault(theObjectClass, "");
if (isMomFederate(className)) {
// One MOM instance per joined federate. Not an entity — this is the
// RTI telling us who else is in the federation.
momInstances.add(id);
log.info("A federate is joined to this federation (details follow if the RTI "
+ "reports them): {}", objectName);
return;
}
String entityType = classifyEntity(className);
log.info("Discovered entity: {} ({}) class={} type={}", objectName, id, className, entityType);
// The class name is kept alongside the bucket: an object is discovered at the
// most specific class *we* subscribed to, and only attributes available there
// can ever reach us — so when a field is null, this is the field that says
// whether we were subscribed deeply enough to have received it.
store.discover(id, objectName, entityType,
className == null || className.isEmpty() ? "(unregistered class)" : className);
// Ask for current values immediately. Without this the entity stays a name
// with no position until the publisher next changes something — which, in a
// paused or slow-moving scenario, can be never.
discoveredClasses.put(theObject, theObjectClass);
requestCurrentValues(theObject, theObjectClass);
}
private static boolean isMomFederate(String className) {
return className.endsWith("HLAmanager.HLAfederate");
}
/**
* Map the discovered FOM object class onto the coarse entity type the REST
* API exposes. Instances are discovered at the most specific class this
* federate subscribes to, so NETN_* leaves resolve precisely.
*/
private static String classifyEntity(String className) {
if (className == null || className.isEmpty()) return "unknown";
if (className.contains("AggregateEntity"))    return "aggregate";
if (className.contains("Aircraft"))           return "aircraft";
if (className.contains("Human"))              return "human";
if (className.contains("Lifeform"))           return "human";
if (className.contains("Vessel"))             return "vessel";
if (className.contains("Spacecraft"))         return "spacecraft";
if (className.contains("Vehicle")
|| className.contains("Platform"))    return "vehicle";
// Discovered at the catch-all. Reported distinctly from "unknown" because
// the two mean different things and both were previously reported as
// "unknown": this is a real entity discovered at a class we subscribe to,
// whereas "unknown" below means the discovered class was one we never
// registered — i.e. our subscription set does not match this FOM.
if (className.contains("BaseEntity"))         return "entity";
return "unknown";
}
@Override
public void reflectAttributeValues(
ObjectInstanceHandle   theObject,
AttributeHandleValueMap theAttributes,
byte[]                 userSuppliedTag,
OrderType              sentOrdering,
TransportationTypeHandle theTransport,
SupplementalReflectInfo supplementalReflectInfo)
throws FederateInternalError {
String id = theObject.toString();
if (momInstances.contains(id)) {
logMomFederate(theAttributes);
return;
}
// Remember which subscribed attributes this instance has now reported, so
// the periodic refresh can stop asking for them. Recorded before decoding:
// the attribute arrived whether or not we could decode its value, and
// re-requesting it would not fix a decode fault.
receivedAttributes.computeIfAbsent(theObject,
k -> java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet()).addAll(theAttributes.keySet());
for (var entry : theAttributes.entrySet()) {
String attrName = attrNames.getOrDefault(entry.getKey(),
entry.getKey().toString());
byte[] value = entry.getValue();
try {
switch (attrName) {
case "Spatial"          -> decodeSpatial(id, value);
case "EntityType"       -> store.updateEntityType(id, decodeEntityType(value));
case "EntityIdentifier" -> decodeEntityIdentifier(id, value);
case "Marking"          -> store.updateMarking(id, decodeMarking(value));
case "AggregateMarking" -> store.updateMarking(id, decodeMarking(value));
// NETN Callsign is an HLAunicodeString (4-byte count then
// UTF-16BE), not a null-terminated UTF-8 string.
case "Callsign"         -> store.updateCallsign(id, decodeUnicodeString(value));
// Both spellings. netn2_2010 and the v4 merged FOM call it
// Symbol; NETN3 and SWORD_FOM.xml call it SymbolId. We subscribe
// to both, so we must decode both — a name handled here but not
// subscribed is inert, whereas the reverse silently drops data.
case "Symbol", "SymbolId" -> store.updateSymbol(id, decodeString(value));
case "DamageState"      -> store.updateDamageState(id, decodeDamageState(value));
case "ForceIdentifier"  -> store.updateForce(id, decodeForce(value));
case "AggregateState"   -> store.updateAggregateState(id, decodeAggregateState(value));
case "Posture"          -> store.updatePosture(id, decodeString(value));
case "Mission"          -> store.updateMission(id, decodeString(value));
case "Activity"         -> store.updateActivity(id, decodeString(value));
case "CombatValue"      -> store.updateCombatValue(id, decodeInt32(value));
case "Echelon"          -> store.updateEchelon(id, decodeString(value));
case "IsPC"             -> store.updateIsCommandPost(id, decodeBoolean(value));
// netn2_2010 spells it UniqueID; NETN3 and SWORD_FOM.xml spell it
// UniqueId. Handling only one is why no entity ever carried a
// UUID against a netn2 federation — and no UUID means no task,
// since every NETN task names its Taskee by one.
case "UniqueId", "UniqueID" -> store.updateUniqueId(id, decodeUUID(value));
case "SupportedActions" -> logSupportedActions(id, value);
default                 -> {} // ignore
}
} catch (Exception e) {
log.warn("Failed to decode attr '{}' for entity {}: {}",
attrName, id, e.getMessage());
}
}
}
/**
* Time-stamp-order variant. Only delivered if this federate becomes
* time-constrained (see BRIDGE_TIME_MANAGEMENT); implemented so enabling
* time management cannot silently stop attribute updates from arriving.
*/
@Override

AttributeHandleValueMap theAttributes,
byte[]                 userSuppliedTag,
OrderType              sentOrdering,
TransportationTypeHandle theTransport,

OrderType              receivedOrdering,
SupplementalReflectInfo supplementalReflectInfo)
throws FederateInternalError {
reflectAttributeValues(theObject, theAttributes, userSuppliedTag, sentOrdering,
theTransport, supplementalReflectInfo);
}

// ── Interactions ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@Override
public void receiveInteraction(
InteractionClassHandle   theInteraction,
ParameterHandleValueMap  theParameters,
byte[]                   userSuppliedTag,
OrderType                sentOrdering,
TransportationTypeHandle theTransport,
SupplementalReceiveInfo  supplementalReceiveInfo)
throws FederateInternalError {
String interName = interNames.getOrDefault(theInteraction,
theInteraction.toString());
// Count everything, including classes handled below: "did we receive any
// of these at all, and when was the last one?" is the first question
// asked when a report is expected and not seen.
diagnostics.recordInteractionReceived(interName);
for (var entry : theParameters.entrySet()) {
String paramName = paramNames.getOrDefault(entry.getKey(),
entry.getKey().toString());
log.debug("  param {}: {} bytes", paramName, entry.getValue().length);
}
// The acknowledgement for anything this bridge tasked: it says whether
// the simulator accepted, refused or completed the task, keyed by the
// taskId returned from the REST tasking endpoints. v2-era calls this
// ETR_TaskManagement.TaskStatusReport; v4 moved it to the root as
// ETR_TaskStatus and renamed its TaskId parameter to Task.
if (interName.endsWith(".TaskStatusReport") || interName.endsWith(".ETR_TaskStatus")) {
logTaskStatus(theInteraction, theParameters);
return;
}
// v4 only. Sent by any NETN-SMC compliant federate for every entity
// control action it receives, whether or not it supports it — so silence
// here means nothing is listening, which is a different problem from a
// task being refused.
if (interName.endsWith(".SMC_Response")) {
logSmcResponse(theInteraction, theParameters);
return;
}
log.info("Received interaction: {}", interName);
}
/** Time-stamp-order variant — see the note on reflectAttributeValues. */
@Override
public void receiveInteraction(
InteractionClassHandle   theInteraction,
ParameterHandleValueMap  theParameters,
byte[]                   userSuppliedTag,
OrderType                sentOrdering,
TransportationTypeHandle theTransport,
LogicalTime              theTime,
OrderType                receivedOrdering,
SupplementalReceiveInfo  supplementalReceiveInfo)
throws FederateInternalError {
receiveInteraction(theInteraction, theParameters, userSuppliedTag, sentOrdering,
theTransport, supplementalReceiveInfo);
}
@Override
public void receiveInteraction(
InteractionClassHandle   theInteraction,
ParameterHandleValueMap  theParameters,
byte[]                   userSuppliedTag,
OrderType                sentOrdering,
TransportationTypeHandle theTransport,
LogicalTime              theTime,
OrderType                receivedOrdering,
MessageRetractionHandle  retractionHandle,
SupplementalReceiveInfo  supplementalReceiveInfo)
throws FederateInternalError {
receiveInteraction(theInteraction, theParameters, userSuppliedTag, sentOrdering,
theTransport, supplementalReceiveInfo);
}
/**
* The RTI/FedPro session has gone away. Without this the bridge keeps
* reporting healthy after the connection drops, because nothing else ever
* clears the connected flag.
*/
@Override
public void connectionLost(String faultDescription) throws FederateInternalError {
log.error("Connection to the RTI lost: {}", faultDescription);
store.setConnected(false);
if (connectionLostHandler != null) {
connectionLostHandler.run();
}
}
private void logTaskStatus(InteractionClassHandle theInteraction,
ParameterHandleValueMap theParameters) {
// v2 names the task reference TaskId, v4 names it Task. Try both rather
// than branching on the class name: a federation running a merged FOM can
// define either, and getting "task ? is Accepted" in the log would waste
// the one message that says whether tasking works at all.
String taskId = firstDecodable(theInteraction, theParameters,
this::decodeUUID, "TaskId", "Task");
String status = firstDecodable(theInteraction, theParameters,
this::decodeTaskStatus, "TaskStatus");
log.info("Task status: task {} is {}", taskId, status);
// Attributed to the task if we sent it. An unmatched one means another
// federate is tasking, which is itself worth knowing: the classes work.
diagnostics.recordAcknowledgement(taskId, "task status: " + status);
}
/**
* v4 {@code SMC_Response} — {Action (the UniqueId of the action being
* answered), Status (HLAboolean)}.
*/
private void logSmcResponse(InteractionClassHandle theInteraction,
ParameterHandleValueMap theParameters) {
String action = firstDecodable(theInteraction, theParameters,
this::decodeUUID, "Action");
String status = firstDecodable(theInteraction, theParameters,
value -> decodeInt32(value) != 0 ? "accepted" : "rejected/failed", "Status");
log.info("SMC_Response: action {} was {}", action, status);
// SMC_Response.Action references the UniqueId of the interaction it
// answers, which is the id recorded when the task was sent.
diagnostics.recordAcknowledgement(action, "SMC_Response: " + status);
}
/**
* Decode the first of several candidate parameter names that this FOM
* defines and the interaction actually carried.
*/
private String firstDecodable(InteractionClassHandle theInteraction,
ParameterHandleValueMap theParameters,
java.util.function.Function<byte[], String> decoder,
String... parameterNames) {
for (String parameterName : parameterNames) {
try {
byte[] raw = theParameters.get(
rtiAmbassador.getParameterHandle(theInteraction, parameterName));
if (raw != null) {
return decoder.apply(raw);
}
} catch (Exception e) {
log.debug("Parameter {} not present here: {}", parameterName, e.getMessage());
}
}
return "?";
}
/**
* Report a MOM federate record. {@code objectsRegistered} and
* {@code updatesSent} are the useful ones when entities are missing: a
* federate registering objects while this bridge discovers nothing means the
* objects are published as a class we do not subscribe to, whereas zero
* registered means that federate is not publishing at all.
*/
private void logMomFederate(AttributeHandleValueMap theAttributes) {
StringBuilder details = new StringBuilder();
// Also kept, not just logged: /diagnostics/federates answers "is anyone
// else here, and are they publishing?" from the RTI's own accounting
// rather than from our decode of their objects.
String name = null;
String type = null;
String host = null;
Integer objectsRegistered = null;
Integer updatesSent = null;
List<String> fomModules = null;
for (var entry : theAttributes.entrySet()) {
String attrName = attrNames.getOrDefault(entry.getKey(), "");
byte[] value = entry.getValue();
try {
switch (attrName) {
case "HLAfederateName" -> {
name = decodeUnicodeString(value);
details.append(" name='").append(name).append('\'');
}
case "HLAfederateType" -> {
type = decodeUnicodeString(value);
details.append(" type='").append(type).append('\'');
}
case "HLAfederateHost" -> {
host = decodeUnicodeString(value);
details.append(" host='").append(host).append('\'');
}
case "HLAFOMmoduleDesignatorList" -> {
fomModules = decodeStringArray(value);
details.append(" fomModules=").append(fomModules);
}
case "HLAobjectInstancesRegistered" -> {
objectsRegistered = decodeInt32(value);
details.append(" objectsRegistered=").append(objectsRegistered);
}
case "HLAupdatesSent" -> {
updatesSent = decodeInt32(value);
details.append(" updatesSent=").append(updatesSent);
}
default -> { }
}
} catch (Exception e) {
log.debug("Could not decode MOM attribute {}: {}", attrName, e.getMessage());
}
}
log.info("Federate:{}", details.length() == 0 ? " (no details reported)" : details);
diagnostics.recordFederate(name, type, host, objectsRegistered, updatesSent, fomModules);
}
/**
* HLAvariableArray of HLAunicodeString: a 4-byte element count, then each
* element as its own 4-byte character count plus UTF-16BE characters, padded
* to the 4-byte element boundary between entries.
*/
private java.util.List<String> decodeStringArray(byte[] value) {
java.util.List<String> out = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
if (value == null || value.length < 4) return out;
int count = decodeInt32Raw(value, 0);
int offset = 4;
for (int i = 0; i < count && offset + 4 <= value.length; i++) {
int chars = decodeInt32Raw(value, offset);
offset += 4;
int bytes = Math.min(Math.max(chars, 0) * 2, value.length - offset);
if (bytes < 0) break;
out.add(new String(value, offset, bytes, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_16BE));
offset += bytes;
while (offset % 4 != 0 && offset < value.length) offset++;
}
return out;
}
/** HLAunicodeString: 4-byte big-endian character count, then UTF-16BE. */
private String decodeUnicodeString(byte[] value) {
if (value == null || value.length < 4) return "";
int chars = decodeInt32Raw(value, 0);
int bytes = Math.min(chars * 2, value.length - 4);
if (bytes <= 0) return "";
return new String(value, 4, bytes, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_16BE);
}
/**
* {@code EntityControlActionEnum} values, collected from the v4 modules that
* extend it: NETN-ETR (tasks), NETN-MRM (100s), NETN-COM (200s), NETN-SE
* (400s) and NETN-LOG (600s).
*
* <p>Note that the enumerator for ChangeRulesOfEngagement is still named
* SetRulesOfEngagement (41) — the interaction was renamed in v4 but the
* enumerator was not.
*/
private static final Map<Integer, String> ENTITY_CONTROL_ACTIONS = Map.ofEntries(
Map.entry(10, "MagicMove"),
Map.entry(18, "Detach"),
Map.entry(20, "DirectFire"),
Map.entry(21, "EnterFacility"),
Map.entry(22, "IndirectFire"),
Map.entry(24, "FollowEntity"),
Map.entry(25, "MoveInDirection"),
Map.entry(26, "MoveIntoFormation"),
Map.entry(28, "MoveToLocation"),
Map.entry(29, "MoveByRoute"),
Map.entry(30, "Attach"),
Map.entry(31, "Observe"),
Map.entry(32, "OperateCheckpoint"),
Map.entry(33, "Patrol"),
Map.entry(34, "OperateObservationPost"),
Map.entry(35, "ChangeAltitude"),
Map.entry(36, "ChangeSpeed"),
Map.entry(39, "StopAtSideOfRoad"),
Map.entry(40, "ChangeHeading"),
Map.entry(41, "ChangeRulesOfEngagement"),
Map.entry(42, "OtherActivity"),
Map.entry(100, "Aggregate"),
Map.entry(101, "Disaggregate"),
Map.entry(102, "Divide"),
Map.entry(103, "Merge"),
Map.entry(200, "DisruptCommunication"),
Map.entry(201, "SetTransmitterStatus"),
Map.entry(401, "ClearEngineering"),
Map.entry(402, "CreateBreach"),
Map.entry(403, "EstablishCheckpoint"),
Map.entry(404, "LayMinefield"),
Map.entry(405, "CreateObstacle"),
Map.entry(406, "EstablishObservationPost"),
Map.entry(601, "Resupply"),
Map.entry(602, "Repair"),
Map.entry(603, "Transport"),
Map.entry(604, "Reset"),
Map.entry(605, "SetSupplies"),
Map.entry(606, "SetPersonnelStatus"),
Map.entry(607, "SetEquipmentStatus"));
/** Entities whose SupportedActions have already been logged. */
private final java.util.Set<String> loggedSupportedActions =
java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<>());
/**
* Report a v4 entity's {@code SupportedActions} (NETN-SMC).
*
* <p>This is the most useful single line the bridge can log about tasking:
* NETN-SMC says to check it <em>before</em> sending an entity control action,
* and it distinguishes "the simulator will not accept this task" from "the
* task was sent and ignored" without waiting on a response that may never
* come. An empty list means the entity accepts no control actions at all —
* monitor-only, whatever the FOM defines.
*
* <p>Logged once per entity: it is a rarely-changing attribute, and the RTI
* may re-send it on any update.
*/
private void logSupportedActions(String id, byte[] value) {
List<String> actions = decodeEntityControlActions(value);
diagnostics.recordSupportedActions(id, actions);
if (!loggedSupportedActions.add(id)) {
log.debug("Entity {} supports control actions: {}", id, actions);
return;
}
if (actions.isEmpty()) {
log.warn("Entity {} publishes SupportedActions but the list is EMPTY — its simulator "
+ "accepts no NETN entity control actions, so it cannot be tasked.", id);
} else {
log.info("Entity {} supports control actions: {}", id, actions);
}
}
/**
* {@code EntityControlActions} — HLAvariableArray of
* {@code EntityControlActionEnum} (HLAinteger32BE): a 4-byte element count
* followed by 4-byte enumerators. Unknown values are reported numerically
* rather than dropped, since a federation may extend the enumeration with
* modules this bridge does not know.
*/
private List<String> decodeEntityControlActions(byte[] value) {
List<String> actions = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
if (value == null || value.length < 4) return actions;
int count = decodeInt32Raw(value, 0);
for (int i = 0; i < count && 4 + 4 * i + 4 <= value.length; i++) {
int code = decodeInt32Raw(value, 4 + 4 * i);
actions.add(ENTITY_CONTROL_ACTIONS.getOrDefault(code, "action#" + code));
}
return actions;
}
/** TaskStatusEnum32 as defined in the loaded FOM. */
private String decodeTaskStatus(byte[] value) {
return switch (decodeInt32(value)) {
case 0 -> "Accepted";
case 1 -> "Refused";
case 2 -> "Cancelled";
case 3 -> "Executing";
case 4 -> "Completed";
case 5 -> "Error";
default -> "unknown";
};
}

store.setSimTime(t.getValue());
}
}

public void timeRegulationEnabled(LogicalTime theFederateTime)
throws FederateInternalError {
log.info("Time regulation enabled at {}", theFederateTime);
}
@Override

throws FederateInternalError {
