}
// ── Decoders (unchanged from original) ───────────────────────────────────
/**
* Decode the RPR-FOM {@code Spatial} attribute.
*
* <p>{@code SpatialVariantStruct} is a <em>variant record</em>, not a bare
* struct:
*
* <pre>
* offset 0   DeadReckoningAlgorithmEnum8   (1 byte discriminant)
* offset 1-7 padding to the alternative's 8-byte boundary
* offset 8   WorldLocationStruct.X         (float64, geocentric ECEF metres)
* offset 16  WorldLocationStruct.Y
* offset 24  WorldLocationStruct.Z
* offset 32  IsFrozen                      (RPRboolean, 1 byte)
* offset 36  Orientation.Psi               (float32 radians, = heading)
* offset 40  Orientation.Theta
* offset 44  Orientation.Phi
* </pre>
*
* <p>Every alternative (Static, DRM_FPW, DRM_RVW, …) begins with the same
* WorldLocation/IsFrozen/Orientation prefix and differs only in the
* velocity/acceleration fields that follow, so the fields we want sit at
* fixed offsets regardless of the dead-reckoning algorithm in use.
*
* <p>The previous version read three float64s from offset 0, i.e. it took
* the discriminant plus padding as latitude and the real X/Y as
* longitude/altitude. The tell-tale symptom was a longitude of exactly
* 90.0 — {@code atan2(y, x)} with x pinned near zero — and altitudes in the
* millions of metres.
*/
private void decodeSpatial(String id, byte[] value) {
// 8 bytes of discriminant + padding, then 24 bytes of WorldLocation.
if (value == null || value.length < 32) {
log.debug("Spatial attribute too short to decode ({} bytes) for {}",
value == null ? 0 : value.length, id);
return;
}
double x = decodeDouble(value, 8);
double y = decodeDouble(value, 16);
double z = decodeDouble(value, 24);
// An all-zero WorldLocation means the publisher has not set a position
// yet. Converting it would report a point at the centre of the Earth.
if (x == 0.0 && y == 0.0 && z == 0.0) {
log.debug("Spatial for {} has no position set yet.", id);
return;
}
double[] wgs84 = ecefToWgs84(x, y, z);
store.updatePosition(id, wgs84[0], wgs84[1], wgs84[2]);
// Orientation.Psi is the heading, in radians, true north.
if (value.length >= 40) {
float psi = Float.intBitsToFloat(decodeInt32Raw(value, 36));
if (!Float.isNaN(psi) && !Float.isInfinite(psi)) {
double heading = Math.toDegrees(psi) % 360.0;
store.updateHeading(id, heading < 0 ? heading + 360.0 : heading);
}
}
// VelocityVector, three float32 ECEF components immediately after the
// orientation at 48/52/56. Never read before, which is the whole reason
// speedMps has always been null — nothing was decoding it.
//
// Only the dead-reckoned alternatives carry it; the Static alternative
// (discriminant 1) ends after the orientation, so guard on the
// discriminant as well as the length rather than trusting either alone.
int deadReckoningAlgorithm = value[0] & 0xFF;
if (deadReckoningAlgorithm != 1 && value.length >= 60) {
float vx = Float.intBitsToFloat(decodeInt32Raw(value, 48));
float vy = Float.intBitsToFloat(decodeInt32Raw(value, 52));
float vz = Float.intBitsToFloat(decodeInt32Raw(value, 56));
boolean usable = !Float.isNaN(vx) && !Float.isNaN(vy) && !Float.isNaN(vz)
&& !Float.isInfinite(vx) && !Float.isInfinite(vy) && !Float.isInfinite(vz);
if (usable) {
// Magnitude of the geocentric velocity: speed through space, which
// for a ground unit is ground speed to within the vertical
// component. Reported in m/s, as the FOM carries it.
store.updateSpeed(id, Math.sqrt(
(double) vx * vx + (double) vy * vy + (double) vz * vz));
}
}
}
private String decodeEntityType(byte[] value) {
if (value.length < 8) return "0.0.0.0.0.0.0";
int kind     = value[0] & 0xFF;
int domain   = value[1] & 0xFF;
int country  = ((value[2] & 0xFF) << 8) | (value[3] & 0xFF);
int category = value[4] & 0xFF;
int sub      = value[5] & 0xFF;
int specific = value[6] & 0xFF;
int extra    = value[7] & 0xFF;
return kind+"."+domain+"."+country+"."+category+"."+sub+"."+specific+"."+extra;
}
/**
* EntityIdentifierStruct — {{SiteID, ApplicationID}, EntityNumber}.
*
* <p>Six bytes, three big-endian unsigned 16-bit fields, no padding: the
* nested federate record is two shorts and adds no alignment of its own.
* Mirrors {@link HlaEncoder#entityIdentifier(int, int, int)} on the way out.
*
* <p>This is the DIS-side name for an entity, and for a simulator that
* publishes no NETN {@code UniqueId} it is the only name there is. Decoding
* it is what makes MACE and VBS4 entities addressable at all.
*
* <p>A short or absent value is ignored rather than defaulted: 0-0-0 is a
* legal-looking identifier that would silently address nothing.
*/
private void decodeEntityIdentifier(String id, byte[] value) {
if (value == null || value.length < 6) {
log.debug("EntityIdentifier for {} was {} bytes, expected 6 — ignoring.",
id, value == null ? 0 : value.length);
return;
}
int site   = decodeUint16(value, 0);
int app    = decodeUint16(value, 2);
int number = decodeUint16(value, 4);
store.updateEntityIdentifier(id, site, app, number);
}
private int decodeUint16(byte[] b, int offset) {
return ((b[offset] & 0xFF) << 8) | (b[offset + 1] & 0xFF);
}
private String decodeMarking(byte[] value) {
if (value.length < 2) return "";
int len = Math.min(11, value.length - 1);
return new String(value, 1, len).trim().replace("\0", "");
}
private String decodeString(byte[] value) {
if (value == null || value.length == 0) return "";
int len = value.length;
for (int i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
if (value[i] == 0) { len = i; break; }
}
return new String(value, 0, len, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8).trim();
}
private String decodeDamageState(byte[] value) {
if (value.length < 4) return "unknown";
return switch (decodeInt32Raw(value, 0)) {
case 0  -> "no_damage";
case 1  -> "slight_damage";
case 2  -> "moderate_damage";
case 3  -> "destroyed";
default -> "unknown";
};
}
private String decodeForce(byte[] value) {
if (value.length < 1) return "unknown";
return switch (value[0] & 0xFF) {
case 1  -> "friendly";
case 2  -> "opposing";
case 3  -> "neutral";
default -> "other";
};
}
private String decodeAggregateState(byte[] value) {
if (value.length < 1) return "unknown";
return switch (value[0] & 0xFF) {
case 1  -> "aggregated";
case 2  -> "disaggregated";
case 3  -> "fully_disaggregated";
case 4  -> "pseudo_disaggregated";
default -> "unknown";
};
}
private int decodeInt32(byte[] value) {
if (value.length < 4) return 0;
return decodeInt32Raw(value, 0);
}
private int decodeInt32Raw(byte[] b, int offset) {
return ((b[offset]   & 0xFF) << 24)
| ((b[offset+1] & 0xFF) << 16)
| ((b[offset+2] & 0xFF) << 8)
|  (b[offset+3] & 0xFF);
}
private boolean decodeBoolean(byte[] value) {
return value.length > 0 && value[0] != 0;
}
private String decodeUUID(byte[] value) {
if (value.length < 16) return "";
StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
if (i == 4 || i == 6 || i == 8 || i == 10) sb.append('-');
sb.append(String.format("%02x", value[i] & 0xFF));
}
return sb.toString();
}
private double decodeDouble(byte[] b, int offset) {
long bits = 0;
for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
bits = (bits << 8) | (b[offset + i] & 0xFF);
return Double.longBitsToDouble(bits);
}
private double[] ecefToWgs84(double x, double y, double z) {
double a   = 6378137.0;
double e2  = 6.6943799901377997e-3;
double lon = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(y, x));
double p   = Math.sqrt(x*x + y*y);
double lat = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(z, p * (1 - e2)));
for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
double sinLat = Math.sin(Math.toRadians(lat));
double N      = a / Math.sqrt(1 - e2 * sinLat * sinLat);
lat = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(z + e2 * N * sinLat, p));
}
double sinLat = Math.sin(Math.toRadians(lat));
double N      = a / Math.sqrt(1 - e2 * sinLat * sinLat);
double alt    = p / Math.cos(Math.toRadians(lat)) - N;
return new double[]{lat, lon, alt};
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/HlaEncoder.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import hla.rti1516e.RtiFactoryFactory;
import hla.rti1516e.encoding.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
/**
* Encoders for the datatypes used by the NETN / RPR-FOM interactions this
* bridge sends. See {@code cse-hla-bridge/foms/SWORD_FOM.xml} for the
* definitions actually loaded into the federation, and
* {@code NETN-FOM-master/modules/NETN-ETR} for the authoritative semantics.
*
* <p>All encoding goes through the RTI's own {@link EncoderFactory} rather
* than hand-rolled {@link java.nio.ByteBuffer} writes. This matters: HLA
* 1516-2010 fixed records, arrays and variant records have alignment/padding
* rules (each element is padded to its own octet boundary, a variant's
* alternative is padded relative to the discriminant, and so on) that a naive
* "concatenate the bytes" encoder gets wrong. The previous implementation
* concatenated raw bytes and encoded UUIDs as null-terminated UTF-8 strings,
* neither of which the RTI can decode.
*
* <p>Key datatype facts taken from the FOM:
* <ul>
*   <li>{@code UUID} / {@code UuidArrayOfHLAbyte16} / {@code TransactionId}
*       = HLAfixedArray of 16 HLAbyte — 16 raw bytes, <em>not</em> text.</li>
*   <li>{@code WorldLocationStruct} = 3 x HLAfloat64BE, geocentric
*       <em>ECEF metres</em> — not latitude/longitude degrees.</li>
*   <li>{@code GeodeticLocation} = 2 x HLAfloat64BE, lat/lon <em>degrees</em>.</li>
*   <li>{@code Datetime18} = HLAfixedArray of 18 HLAunicodeChar (UTF-16BE),
*       format {@code yyyyMMddHHmmss.SSS}. The all-zero value
*       {@code 00000000000000.000} means "start immediately".</li>
*   <li>{@code Callsign} / {@code Text64} = variable array of HLAunicodeChar,
*       byte-identical to HLAunicodeString.</li>
*   <li>Angles ({@code AngleRadianFloat32}) are <em>radians</em>, not degrees.</li>
*   <li>{@code HLAboolean} = HLAinteger32BE (0/1) while
*       {@code RPRboolean} = HLAoctet (0/1). They are not interchangeable.</li>
* </ul>
*
* <h2>NETN v4 datatypes</h2>
* NETN FOM v4 (NETN-BASE 3.0 and later) replaced several of the types above.
* The differences are all silent-wrong-value traps rather than decode errors,
* so they are listed explicitly:
* <ul>
*   <li>{@code LocationStruct} = 3 x MeterFloat64 {X, Y, Z} in the IEEE
*       1278.1-2012 §1.6.3 <em>world coordinate system</em> — i.e. geocentric
*       ECEF metres, exactly like {@code WorldLocationStruct}. It is
*       <em>not</em> latitude/longitude, despite the name suggesting otherwise;
*       NETN-BASE has separate {@code GeodeticPoint}/{@code GeodeticCircle}
*       types for genuinely geodetic values.</li>
*   <li>{@code DirectionDegreesFloat32} = HLAfloat32BE in <em>degrees</em>
*       clockwise from true north, [0,360). The v2-era
*       {@code AngleRadianFloat32} was radians.</li>
*   <li>{@code EpochTime} = HLAinteger32BE seconds since the Unix epoch. It
*       replaces {@code Datetime18}, which v4 deletes outright.</li>
*   <li>{@code TimeMillisecondInt64} = HLAinteger64BE milliseconds. Durations
*       that were seconds-as-int32 in v2 are milliseconds-as-int64 in v4.</li>
*   <li>{@code SupplyStatusStruct} = {EntityTypeStruct, QuantityFloat64}. The
*       v2 {@code NETN_SupplyStruct} used a float32 quantity.</li>
*   <li>Each task carries one {@code *TaskStruct} HLAfixedRecord instead of
*       flat parameters, so nested records have to be composed as
*       {@link DataElement}s — see {@link #fixedRecord(DataElement...)} — and
*       cannot be concatenated as raw bytes.</li>
* </ul>
*/
public final class HlaEncoder {
/** Datetime18 value meaning "execute immediately". */
public static final String IMMEDIATE = "00000000000000.000";
private static final DateTimeFormatter DT18 =
DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMddHHmmss.SSS").withZone(ZoneOffset.UTC);
// WGS-84 ellipsoid
private static final double WGS84_A  = 6378137.0;
private static final double WGS84_E2 = 6.69437999014e-3;
private final EncoderFactory f;
public HlaEncoder() throws Exception {
// Pure ServiceLoader lookup returning FedPro's OmtEncoderFactory — no
// RTI connection is required, so this is also usable from unit tests.
this.f = RtiFactoryFactory.getRtiFactory().getEncoderFactory();
}
// ── Scalars ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** HLAoctet — also used for every *Enum8 enumeration. */
public byte[] octet(int value) {
return f.createHLAoctet((byte) value).toByteArray();
}
/** HLAinteger16BE (e.g. Integer16). */
public byte[] int16(int value) {
return f.createHLAinteger16BE((short) value).toByteArray();
}
/** HLAinteger32BE — also used for every *Enum32 enumeration and TimeSecInt32. */
public byte[] int32(int value) {
return f.createHLAinteger32BE(value).toByteArray();
}
/** HLAfloat32BE (AngleRadianFloat32, MeterFloat32, VelocityMeterPerSecondFloat32...). */
public byte[] float32(double value) {
return f.createHLAfloat32BE((float) value).toByteArray();
}
/** HLAfloat64BE (MeterFloat64, AltitudeMeterFloat64...). */
public byte[] float64(double value) {
return f.createHLAfloat64BE(value).toByteArray();
}
/** HLAboolean — an HLAinteger32BE enumeration (HLAfalse=0, HLAtrue=1). */
public byte[] hlaBoolean(boolean value) {
return int32(value ? 1 : 0);
}
/** RPRboolean — an HLAoctet enumeration (False=0, True=1). */
public byte[] rprBoolean(boolean value) {
return octet(value ? 1 : 0);
}
/** HLAunicodeString, and the byte-identical Callsign / Text64 types. */
public byte[] unicodeString(String value) {
return f.createHLAunicodeString(value == null ? "" : value).toByteArray();
}
/** HLAASCIIstring. */
public byte[] asciiString(String value) {
return f.createHLAASCIIstring(value == null ? "" : value).toByteArray();
}
// ── Identifiers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* UUID / UuidArrayOfHLAbyte16 / TransactionId — HLAfixedArray of 16 HLAbyte.
*
* @param value a UUID, with or without dashes
* @throws IllegalArgumentException if the value is not a UUID. Callers must
*         resolve an entity's NETN {@code UniqueId} first: an HLA
*         object-instance handle string is not a valid Taskee.
*/
public byte[] uuid(String value) {
return uuid(parseUuid(value));
}
public byte[] uuid(UUID value) {
// HLAfixedArray of 16 HLAbyte: octet-aligned elements, fixed length, so
// the encoding is exactly the 16 raw bytes with no count prefix and no
// padding.
return rawUuid(value);
}
private static byte[] rawUuid(UUID value) {
byte[] raw = new byte[16];
long hi = value.getMostSignificantBits();
long lo = value.getLeastSignificantBits();
for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
raw[i]     = (byte) (hi >>> (56 - 8 * i));
raw[i + 8] = (byte) (lo >>> (56 - 8 * i));
}
return raw;
}
public static UUID parseUuid(String value) {
if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
throw new IllegalArgumentException("Missing UUID");
}
String s = value.trim();
if (s.length() == 32 && s.indexOf('-') < 0) {
s = s.substring(0, 8) + "-" + s.substring(8, 12) + "-" + s.substring(12, 16)
+ "-" + s.substring(16, 20) + "-" + s.substring(20);
}
try {
return UUID.fromString(s);
} catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
throw new IllegalArgumentException("Not a UUID: '" + value + "'");
}
}
/**
* ArrayOfUuid / ArrayOfTaskIds — HLAvariableArray of 16-byte UUIDs:
* a 4-byte element count followed by the octet-aligned elements.
*/
public byte[] arrayOfUuid(List<UUID> ids) {
byte[] out = new byte[4 + 16 * ids.size()];
System.arraycopy(int32(ids.size()), 0, out, 0, 4);
for (int i = 0; i < ids.size(); i++) {
System.arraycopy(rawUuid(ids.get(i)), 0, out, 4 + 16 * i, 16);
}
return out;
}
// ── Time ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* Datetime18 — HLAfixedArray of exactly 18 HLAunicodeChar (UTF-16BE).
*
* @param when null means "immediately" ({@link #IMMEDIATE})
*/
public byte[] datetime18(Instant when) {
String text = (when == null) ? IMMEDIATE : DT18.format(when);
if (text.length() != 18) {
throw new IllegalStateException("Datetime18 must be 18 chars, got: " + text);
}
// HLAfixedArray of 18 HLAunicodeChar (HLAoctetPairBE): 36 bytes of
// UTF-16BE, no length prefix, no padding (the array is 2-aligned and
// every element is 2 bytes).
return text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_16BE);
}
/** ClockTimeStruct — {Hours (int32), TimePastTheHour (uint32 DIS timestamp units)}. */
public byte[] clockTime(Instant when) {
long epochSeconds = when.getEpochSecond();
int hours = (int) (epochSeconds / 3600);
double remainderNanos = (epochSeconds % 3600) * 1_000_000_000.0 + when.getNano();
long units = (long) (remainderNanos / 3_600_000_000_000.0 * 2147483647.0);
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
record.add(f.createHLAinteger32BE(hours));
record.add(f.createHLAinteger32BE((int) units));
return record.toByteArray();
}
// ── Geometry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** WorldLocationStruct — geocentric ECEF metres {X, Y, Z}. */
public byte[] worldLocation(double latitudeDegrees, double longitudeDegrees, double altitudeMeters) {
return worldLocationRecord(latitudeDegrees, longitudeDegrees, altitudeMeters).toByteArray();
}
private HLAfixedRecord worldLocationRecord(double lat, double lon, double alt) {
double[] ecef = wgs84ToEcef(lat, lon, alt);
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
record.add(f.createHLAfloat64BE(ecef[0]));
record.add(f.createHLAfloat64BE(ecef[1]));
record.add(f.createHLAfloat64BE(ecef[2]));
return record;
}
/** ArrayOfWorldLocationStruct — HLAvariableArray of WorldLocationStruct. */
public byte[] arrayOfWorldLocation(List<double[]> latLonAlt) {
HLAfixedRecord[] elements = new HLAfixedRecord[latLonAlt.size()];
for (int i = 0; i < latLonAlt.size(); i++) {
double[] p = latLonAlt.get(i);
elements[i] = worldLocationRecord(p[0], p[1], p.length > 2 ? p[2] : 0.0);
}
HLAvariableArray<HLAfixedRecord> array =
f.createHLAvariableArray(index -> f.createHLAfixedRecord(), elements);
return array.toByteArray();
}
/** GeodeticLocation — {Latitude, Longitude} in degrees. */
private HLAfixedRecord geodeticLocation(double latitudeDegrees, double longitudeDegrees) {
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
record.add(f.createHLAfloat64BE(latitudeDegrees));
record.add(f.createHLAfloat64BE(longitudeDegrees));
return record;
}
/** GeodeticQuadrangle — {Point1, Point2}, i.e. a lat/lon bounding box. */
public byte[] geodeticQuadrangle(double lat1, double lon1, double lat2, double lon2) {
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
record.add(geodeticLocation(lat1, lon1));
record.add(geodeticLocation(lat2, lon2));
return record.toByteArray();
}
/**
* AreaVariantStruct discriminated on GeoLocationTypeEnum32, using the
* Circle alternative (=2): {CenterPoint (GeodeticLocation), Radius (MeterFloat32)}.
*/
public byte[] areaCircle(double latitudeDegrees, double longitudeDegrees, double radiusMeters) {
HLAfixedRecord circle = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
circle.add(geodeticLocation(latitudeDegrees, longitudeDegrees));
circle.add(f.createHLAfloat32BE((float) radiusMeters));
// Reuse the exact discriminant instance for both setVariant and
// setDiscriminant: variant lookup is by object, so this avoids relying
// on the RTI encoder's equals()/hashCode() for HLAinteger32BE.
HLAinteger32BE circleDiscriminant = f.createHLAinteger32BE(2); // Circle
HLAvariantRecord<HLAinteger32BE> variant = f.createHLAvariantRecord(circleDiscriminant);
variant.setVariant(circleDiscriminant, circle);
variant.setDiscriminant(circleDiscriminant);
return variant.toByteArray();
}
/** WGS-84 geodetic (degrees, metres) to geocentric ECEF (metres). */
public static double[] wgs84ToEcef(double latitudeDegrees, double longitudeDegrees, double altitudeMeters) {
double lat = Math.toRadians(latitudeDegrees);
double lon = Math.toRadians(longitudeDegrees);
double sinLat = Math.sin(lat);
double cosLat = Math.cos(lat);
double n = WGS84_A / Math.sqrt(1.0 - WGS84_E2 * sinLat * sinLat);
double x = (n + altitudeMeters) * cosLat * Math.cos(lon);
double y = (n + altitudeMeters) * cosLat * Math.sin(lon);
double z = (n * (1.0 - WGS84_E2) + altitudeMeters) * sinLat;
return new double[]{x, y, z};
}
// ── DIS / RPR structures ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* EntityTypeStruct from a dotted SISO-REF-010 type, e.g.
* {@code "6.2.225.1.0.0.0"} (kind.domain.country.category.subcategory.specific.extra).
*/
public byte[] entityType(String dotted) {
return entityTypeRecord(dotted).toByteArray();
}
private HLAfixedRecord entityTypeRecord(String dotted) {
int[] p = new int[7];
String[] parts = (dotted == null ? "" : dotted).split("\\.");
for (int i = 0; i < 7 && i < parts.length; i++) {
try {
p[i] = Integer.parseInt(parts[i].trim());
} catch (NumberFormatException e) {
throw new IllegalArgumentException("Not a DIS entity type: '" + dotted + "'");
}
}
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
record.add(f.createHLAoctet((byte) p[0]));          // EntityKind
record.add(f.createHLAoctet((byte) p[1]));          // Domain
record.add(f.createHLAinteger16BE((short) p[2]));   // CountryCode
record.add(f.createHLAoctet((byte) p[3]));          // Category
record.add(f.createHLAoctet((byte) p[4]));          // Subcategory
record.add(f.createHLAoctet((byte) p[5]));          // Specific
record.add(f.createHLAoctet((byte) p[6]));          // Extra
return record;
}
/** NETN_SupplyStruct — {SupplyType (EntityTypeStruct), Quantity (float32)}. */
public byte[] supply(String dottedSupplyType, double quantity) {
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
record.add(entityTypeRecord(dottedSupplyType));
record.add(f.createHLAfloat32BE((float) quantity));
return record.toByteArray();
}
/** EntityIdentifierStruct — {{SiteID, ApplicationID}, EntityNumber}. */
public byte[] entityIdentifier(int siteId, int applicationId, int entityNumber) {
HLAfixedRecord federate = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
federate.add(f.createHLAinteger16BE((short) siteId));
federate.add(f.createHLAinteger16BE((short) applicationId));
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
record.add(federate);
record.add(f.createHLAinteger16BE((short) entityNumber));
return record.toByteArray();
}
// ══ NETN v4 ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 collapses each task's parameters into a single TaskParameters fixed
// record, so encoding is composition of nested records rather than one
// parameter per value. Everything below returns DataElement rather than
// byte[] because nesting has to preserve each field's octet boundary:
// HLAfixedRecord pads before a field to that field's own alignment, which
// is information a byte[] no longer carries.
// ── v4 top-level parameter values ─────────────────────────────────────────
/** EpochTime — HLAinteger32BE seconds since the Unix epoch. */
public byte[] epochTime(Instant when) {
return int32((int) (when == null ? Instant.now() : when).getEpochSecond());
}
// ── v4 element factories ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Nested values are built as elements and assembled with fixedRecord(...) at
// the call site, so a task's TaskParameters struct reads in the same field
// order as the FOM defines it.
public DataElement octetEl(int value)      { return f.createHLAoctet((byte) value); }
public DataElement int16El(int value)      { return f.createHLAinteger16BE((short) value); }
public DataElement int32El(int value)      { return f.createHLAinteger32BE(value); }
public DataElement int64El(long value)     { return f.createHLAinteger64BE(value); }
public DataElement float32El(double value) { return f.createHLAfloat32BE((float) value); }
public DataElement float64El(double value) { return f.createHLAfloat64BE(value); }
public DataElement stringEl(String value)  { return f.createHLAunicodeString(value == null ? "" : value); }
/** HLAboolean — an HLAinteger32BE enumeration, not an octet. */
public DataElement booleanEl(boolean value) { return f.createHLAinteger32BE(value ? 1 : 0); }
/** TimeMillisecondInt64. */
public DataElement millisEl(long milliseconds) { return int64El(milliseconds); }
/** DirectionDegreesFloat32, normalised into [0,360). */
public DataElement directionDegreesEl(double degrees) { return float32El(normaliseDegrees(degrees)); }
/**
* UUID — HLAfixedArray of 16 HLAbyte, octet aligned.
*
* <p>Built from the RTI's own HLAbyte elements rather than from a wrapper
* around the 16 raw bytes. A custom {@link DataElement} would work only if
* the encoder's HLAfixedRecord consults {@code getOctetBoundary()} the way
* this one expects; going through the standard array type means the
* alignment behaviour is the RTI's problem, not ours. Sixteen short-lived
* objects per UUID is irrelevant at REST tasking rates.
*/
public DataElement uuidEl(UUID value) {
byte[] raw = rawUuid(value);
HLAbyte[] elements = new HLAbyte[raw.length];
for (int i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {
elements[i] = f.createHLAbyte(raw[i]);
}
return f.createHLAfixedArray(elements);
}
public DataElement uuidEl(String value) { return uuidEl(parseUuid(value)); }
/**
* The all-zero UUID. Every v4 UUID field that means "unspecified" — an
* absent network, an unknown tasker — uses this rather than being omitted,
* because a field inside a fixed record cannot be left out.
*/
public DataElement nilUuidEl() { return uuidEl(new UUID(0L, 0L)); }
/** EntityTypeStruct from a dotted SISO-REF-010 type. */
public DataElement entityTypeEl(String dotted) { return entityTypeRecord(dotted); }
/** LocationStruct as a nested element (geocentric ECEF metres). */
public DataElement locationEl(double latitudeDegrees, double longitudeDegrees, double altitudeMeters) {
return worldLocationRecord(latitudeDegrees, longitudeDegrees, altitudeMeters);
}
/** LocationStructArray — HLAvariableArray of LocationStruct. */
public DataElement locationArrayEl(List<double[]> latLonAlt) {
HLAfixedRecord[] elements = new HLAfixedRecord[latLonAlt.size()];
for (int i = 0; i < latLonAlt.size(); i++) {
double[] p = latLonAlt.get(i);
elements[i] = worldLocationRecord(p[0], p[1], p.length > 2 ? p[2] : 0.0);
}
return f.createHLAvariableArray(index -> f.createHLAfixedRecord(), elements);
}
/**
* ArrayOfWaypoints — HLAvariableArray of
* Waypoint{Location (LocationStruct), Speed (float32 m/s),
* SegmentMaxWidth (float32 m)}.
*
* @param speedMetersPerSecond speed to reach each waypoint; 0 leaves the
*        choice to the simulator
* @param segmentMaxWidthMeters 0 means no width constraint
*/
public DataElement waypointArrayEl(List<double[]> latLonAlt,
double speedMetersPerSecond,
double segmentMaxWidthMeters) {
HLAfixedRecord[] elements = new HLAfixedRecord[latLonAlt.size()];
for (int i = 0; i < latLonAlt.size(); i++) {
double[] p = latLonAlt.get(i);
HLAfixedRecord waypoint = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
waypoint.add(worldLocationRecord(p[0], p[1], p.length > 2 ? p[2] : 0.0));
waypoint.add(f.createHLAfloat32BE((float) speedMetersPerSecond));
waypoint.add(f.createHLAfloat32BE((float) segmentMaxWidthMeters));
elements[i] = waypoint;
}
return f.createHLAvariableArray(index -> f.createHLAfixedRecord(), elements);
}
/**
* RoundStruct — {WeaponType, MunitionType (both EntityTypeStruct),
* QuantityFired (int16), RateOfFire (float32)}.
*
* <p>All-zero types and quantities mean "unspecified, simulator decides",
* which is the only honest default: DIS weapon/munition enumerations are
* scenario-specific and guessing one produces a plausible-looking but wrong
* engagement.
*/
public DataElement roundEl(String dottedWeaponType,
String dottedMunitionType,
int quantityFired,
double roundsPerSecond) {
return recordEl(
entityTypeEl(dottedWeaponType == null ? "0.0.0.0.0.0.0" : dottedWeaponType),
entityTypeEl(dottedMunitionType == null ? "0.0.0.0.0.0.0" : dottedMunitionType),
int16El(quantityFired),
float32El(roundsPerSecond));
}
/** Compose a TaskParameters (or any other) HLAfixedRecord as a value. */
public byte[] fixedRecord(DataElement... fields) {
return recordEl(fields).toByteArray();
}
/**
* Compose an HLAfixedRecord as a nested element, for structs that contain
* structs — v4 {@code CreateObstacleTaskStruct} embeds an
* {@code EngineeringTaskStruct}, for example.
*/
public HLAfixedRecord recordEl(DataElement... fields) {
HLAfixedRecord record = f.createHLAfixedRecord();
for (DataElement field : fields) {
record.add(field);
}
return record;
}
// ── Geometry helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* Approximate a circle as a closed polygon of lat/lon/alt points, for the
* v4 {@code Area} fields that are a {@code LocationStructArray} rather than
* a centre and radius.
*
* <p>The first point is not repeated at the end. NETN-BASE describes these
* arrays as the vertices of an area rather than as a path, so the closing
* edge is implied; whether a given simulator also tolerates a repeated first
* vertex is untested here.
*/
public static List<double[]> circleToPolygon(double latitudeDegrees,
double longitudeDegrees,
double radiusMeters,
double altitudeMeters,
int points) {
int n = Math.max(3, points);
List<double[]> polygon = new java.util.ArrayList<>(n);
double dLat = radiusMeters / 111_320.0;
double cosLat = Math.max(0.01, Math.cos(Math.toRadians(latitudeDegrees)));
double dLon = radiusMeters / (111_320.0 * cosLat);
for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
double angle = 2.0 * Math.PI * i / n;
polygon.add(new double[]{
latitudeDegrees + dLat * Math.sin(angle),
longitudeDegrees + dLon * Math.cos(angle),
altitudeMeters});
}
return polygon;
}
private static double normaliseDegrees(double degrees) {
double d = degrees % 360.0;
return d < 0 ? d + 360.0 : d;
}
/**
* Uppercase hex, space-separated per byte, for diagnostics.
*
* <p>Exists so that "here is exactly what we put on the wire" can be handed to
* another federate's owner and compared against what their decoder reads. An
* ECEF-versus-geodetic disagreement is invisible in decimal — both sides
* believe they are right — but obvious once you look at the bytes.
*/
public static String hex(byte[] bytes) {
if (bytes == null) {
return "";
}
StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(bytes.length * 3);
for (int i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
if (i > 0) {
out.append(' ');
}
out.append(String.format("%02X", bytes[i]));
}
return out.toString();
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/PortProbe.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import javax.net.ssl.SNIHostName;
import javax.net.ssl.SSLContext;
import javax.net.ssl.SSLParameters;
import javax.net.ssl.SSLSocket;
import javax.net.ssl.SSLSocketFactory;
import javax.net.ssl.TrustManager;
import javax.net.ssl.X509TrustManager;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.security.cert.Certificate;
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate;
import java.util.List;
/**
* Optional diagnostic: probes a fixed list of ports for the currently
* configured CSE_ENVIRONMENT, directly from wherever this pod actually
* runs, to get a real open/closed map. Probing from this pod's own network
* position is meaningful because it is the actual client attempting to
* reach the CSE-Prep firewall/ports — probing from an unrelated machine
* would only ever test that machine's own egress rules, not the target's.
*
* Controlled by bridge.port-probe-enabled / PORT_PROBE_ENABLED (default
* true) so it can be turned off without a code change.
*/
final class PortProbe {
private PortProbe() {}
// App codes are the same across every environment (only the leading
// environment digit in 30{ENVIRONMENT}{APPLICATION} changes) — see
// EnvironmentPorts.java. 22 (FedPro UDP) is intentionally omitted —
// plain Socket connect is TCP-only.
private static final int[] APP_CODES = {10, 11, 12, 20, 21, 24, 25};
private static final String[] TCP_LABELS = {
"crc", "crc-ui", "crc-registry", "fedpro-tcp", "fedpro-tls", "fedpro-ws", "fedpro-wss"
};
private static final int CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000;
static void runAndLog(Logger logger, String host, int environmentNumber, String tlsSniHostname) {
logger.info("── Port probe starting for {} (environment {}) ──", host, environmentNumber);
int tlsPort = 0;
for (int i = 0; i < APP_CODES.length; i++) {
// 30{ENVIRONMENT:1}{APPLICATION:2} — e.g. env=1, appCode=20 -> 30120
int port = 30_000 + environmentNumber * 100 + APP_CODES[i];
if (APP_CODES[i] == 21) {
tlsPort = port;
}
probeTcp(logger, host, port, TCP_LABELS[i]);
}
if (tlsPort > 0) {
probeTls(logger, host, tlsPort, tlsSniHostname);
}
logger.info("── Port probe complete ──");
}
private static void probeTcp(Logger logger, String host, int port, String label) {
long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
// host/port are fixed internal config (bridge.fedpro-address host + a
// hardcoded diagnostic port list), never user-supplied input — this is
// a one-off outbound connectivity probe, not a request proxy.
InetSocketAddress target = new InetSocketAddress(host, port); // nosemgrep
try (Socket socket = new Socket()) {
socket.connect(target, CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS);
logger.info("Port probe {}:{} ({}) -> OPEN ({}ms)",
host, port, label, System.currentTimeMillis() - start);
} catch (Exception e) {
logger.info("Port probe {}:{} ({}) -> {} — {} ({}ms)",
host, port, label, e.getClass().getSimpleName(), e.getMessage(),
System.currentTimeMillis() - start);
}
}
/** TLS handshake probe with SNI — the Java equivalent of `openssl s_client -servername ...`. */
private static void probeTls(Logger logger, String host, int port, String sniHostname) {
long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
try {
TrustManager trustAllCerts = new X509TrustManager() {
public void checkClientTrusted(X509Certificate[] chain, String authType) {}
public void checkServerTrusted(X509Certificate[] chain, String authType) {}
public X509Certificate[] getAcceptedIssuers() { return new X509Certificate[0]; }
};
// Pin to TLSv1.2/1.3 explicitly (never negotiate older, weaker protocol
// versions) — this is a diagnostic handshake probe, not just a fixed
// "TLS" alias that could quietly allow a downgraded connection.
SSLContext sslContext = SSLContext.getInstance("TLSv1.3");
sslContext.init(null, new TrustManager[]{trustAllCerts}, new SecureRandom());
try (Socket rawSocket = new Socket()) {
// host/port are fixed internal config, never user-supplied input —
// this is a one-off outbound connectivity probe.
rawSocket.connect(new InetSocketAddress(host, port), CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS); // nosemgrep
SSLSocketFactory factory = sslContext.getSocketFactory();
try (SSLSocket sslSocket = (SSLSocket) factory.createSocket(rawSocket, host, port, true)) {
sslSocket.setEnabledProtocols(new String[]{"TLSv1.2", "TLSv1.3"});
if (sniHostname != null && !sniHostname.isBlank()) {
SSLParameters params = sslSocket.getSSLParameters();
params.setServerNames(List.of(new SNIHostName(sniHostname)));
sslSocket.setSSLParameters(params);
}
sslSocket.setSoTimeout(CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS);
sslSocket.startHandshake();
Certificate[] peerCerts = sslSocket.getSession().getPeerCertificates();
String subject = (peerCerts.length > 0 && peerCerts[0] instanceof X509Certificate x509)
? x509.getSubjectX500Principal().getName()
: "(no cert)";
logger.info("TLS probe {}:{} (sni={}) -> HANDSHAKE OK, peer cert subject={} ({}ms)",
host, port, sniHostname, subject, System.currentTimeMillis() - start);
}
}
} catch (Exception e) {
logger.info("TLS probe {}:{} (sni={}) -> {} — {} ({}ms)",
host, port, sniHostname, e.getClass().getSimpleName(), e.getMessage(),
System.currentTimeMillis() - start);
}
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/SmcV4Dialect.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import hla.rti1516e.InteractionClassHandle;
import hla.rti1516e.ParameterHandleValueMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
/**
* NETN v4 tasking — {@code SMC_EntityControl.*}, per NETN-ETR 3.0 / NETN-SMC 1.0.
*
* <h2>What changed from v2</h2>
* <ul>
*   <li>{@code ETR_Root}, {@code ETR_SimCon} and {@code ETR_TaskManagement} are
*       gone. Every task is a subclass of {@code SMC_EntityControl.Task}, and
*       MagicMove — an umpire action in v2 — is now an ordinary task.</li>
*   <li>{@code Taskee} became {@code Entity}, inherited from
*       {@code SMC_EntityControl}.</li>
*   <li>{@code Tasker} changed from a Callsign <em>string</em> to a
*       <em>UUID</em> referencing the commanding entity. Sending a string here
*       decodes as garbage rather than failing, so it is omitted unless
*       {@code TASKER_UUID} is configured.</li>
*   <li>{@code StartWhen} (Datetime18) became {@code StartTime} (EpochTime) and
*       {@code Why} became {@code Annotation}.</li>
*   <li>Every task's own parameters collapse into a single
*       {@code TaskParameters} fixed record whose type differs per task.</li>
*   <li>Durations are {@code TimeMillisecondInt64} milliseconds, not int32
*       seconds.</li>
*   <li>Headings are {@code DirectionDegreesFloat32} degrees, not radians.</li>
*   <li>{@code CancelAllTasks} and {@code CancelSpecifiedTasks} merged into
*       {@code CancelTasks}, where an empty {@code Tasks} array means all.</li>
*   <li>Obstacles, comms disruption and resupply moved out of NETN-ETR into
*       NETN-SE, NETN-COM and NETN-LOG respectively. Those modules are optional,
*       so those three endpoints work only if the federation loaded them —
*       {@link #optional()} exists so the startup log says which.</li>
* </ul>
*
* <p>{@code LocationStruct} is <em>geocentric ECEF metres</em>, not
* latitude/longitude — see {@link HlaEncoder}. That is the one place where the
* name invites a wrong conversion.
*/
class SmcV4Dialect extends BaseDialect {
// ── FOM class names ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
private static final String SMC  = "HLAinteractionRoot.SMC_EntityControl.";
private static final String TASK = SMC + "Task.";
// NETN-ETR 3.0 tasks.
static final String MOVE_TO_LOCATION   = TASK + "MoveToLocation";
static final String MOVE_BY_ROUTE      = TASK + "MoveByRoute";
static final String MOVE_IN_DIRECTION  = TASK + "MoveInDirection";
static final String FOLLOW_ENTITY      = TASK + "FollowEntity";
static final String PATROL             = TASK + "Patrol";
static final String MAGIC_MOVE         = TASK + "MagicMove";
static final String DIRECT_FIRE        = TASK + "DirectFire";
static final String INDIRECT_FIRE      = TASK + "IndirectFire";
static final String CHANGE_ROE         = TASK + "ChangeRulesOfEngagement";
static final String CHANGE_HEADING     = TASK + "ChangeHeading";
static final String CHANGE_SPEED       = TASK + "ChangeSpeed";
static final String CHANGE_ALTITUDE    = TASK + "ChangeAltitude";
static final String STOP_AT_SIDE_OF_ROAD = TASK + "StopAtSideOfRoad";
static final String OBSERVE            = TASK + "Observe";
// Task management (siblings of Task, not subclasses of it).
static final String CANCEL_TASKS        = SMC + "CancelTasks";
static final String REQUEST_TASK_STATUS = SMC + "RequestTaskStatus";
// Tasks that moved to other modules.
static final String CREATE_OBSTACLE       = TASK + "CreateObstacle";        // NETN-SE
static final String CLEAR_ENGINEERING     = TASK + "ClearEngineering";      // NETN-SE
static final String DISRUPT_COMMUNICATION = TASK + "DisruptCommunication";  // NETN-COM
static final String SET_SUPPLIES_STATUS   = SMC + "SetSuppliesStatus";      // NETN-LOG
// NETN-MRM 3.0: entity control actions, no longer addressed to a federate.
static final String MRM_AGGREGATE    = SMC + "Aggregate";
static final String MRM_DISAGGREGATE = SMC + "Disaggregate";
static final String MRM_DIVIDE       = SMC + "Divide";
static final String MRM_MERGE        = SMC + "Merge";
// Reports and acknowledgements.
private static final String REPORT = "HLAinteractionRoot.ETR_Report.";
static final String TASK_STATUS              = "HLAinteractionRoot.ETR_TaskStatus";
static final String SMC_RESPONSE             = "HLAinteractionRoot.SMC_Response";
static final String POSITION_STATUS_REPORT   = REPORT + "PositionStatusReport";
static final String DAMAGE_STATUS_REPORT     = REPORT + "DamageStatusReport";
static final String RESOURCE_STATUS_REPORT   = REPORT + "ResourceStatusReport";
static final String UNDER_ATTACK_REPORT      = REPORT + "UnderAttackStatusReport";
static final String IN_WEAPON_RANGE_REPORT   = REPORT + "InWeaponRangeReport";
static final String OBSERVATION_REPORT       = REPORT + "ObservationReport";
static final String ENTITY_SENSOR_UPDATE     = "HLAinteractionRoot.ETR_SensorEvent.EntitySensorUpdate";
/** Points used to approximate a circular area as a LocationStructArray. */
private static final int AREA_POLYGON_POINTS = 8;
SmcV4Dialect(BridgeFederate federate, Identity identity) {
super(federate, identity);
}
@Override public String id()   { return "v4"; }
@Override public String name() { return "NETN v4 (SMC_EntityControl.Task)"; }
@Override public String detectionClass() { return MOVE_TO_LOCATION; }
/**
* Class names are qualified with the type even though they are fields of it:
* several {@link Op} constants share a name with the field they map to
* (PATROL, MAGIC_MOVE, CANCEL_TASKS, CREATE_OBSTACLE), and while the case
* label and the result expression resolve in different namespaces, reading
* {@code case PATROL -> PATROL} invites the reader to assume a mistake.
*/
@Override
public String classFor(Op op) {
return switch (op) {
case MOVE              -> SmcV4Dialect.MOVE_TO_LOCATION;
case PATROL            -> SmcV4Dialect.PATROL;
case MAGIC_MOVE        -> SmcV4Dialect.MAGIC_MOVE;
case FIRE_AT_LOCATION  -> SmcV4Dialect.INDIRECT_FIRE;
case FIRE_AT_ENTITY    -> SmcV4Dialect.DIRECT_FIRE;
case ROE               -> SmcV4Dialect.CHANGE_ROE;
case CANCEL_TASKS      -> SmcV4Dialect.CANCEL_TASKS;
case INJECT_SUPPLIES   -> SmcV4Dialect.SET_SUPPLIES_STATUS;
case CREATE_OBSTACLE   -> SmcV4Dialect.CREATE_OBSTACLE;
case DISRUPT_COMMS     -> SmcV4Dialect.DISRUPT_COMMUNICATION;
case AGGREGATE         -> SmcV4Dialect.MRM_AGGREGATE;
case DISAGGREGATE      -> SmcV4Dialect.MRM_DISAGGREGATE;
case CHANGE_HEADING    -> SmcV4Dialect.CHANGE_HEADING;
case CHANGE_SPEED      -> SmcV4Dialect.CHANGE_SPEED;
case CHANGE_ALTITUDE   -> SmcV4Dialect.CHANGE_ALTITUDE;
case STOP              -> SmcV4Dialect.STOP_AT_SIDE_OF_ROAD;
};
}
// ── Simple scalar tasks ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Each carries a one-field TaskParameters struct, except StopAtSideOfRoad
// which the FOM gives no parameters at all (TaskDefinitionVariantRecord maps
// it to EmptyTaskStruct).
@Override
public UUID changeHeading(String entityUuid, double headingDegrees) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(CHANGE_HEADING);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge heading change", true);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
// ChangeHeadingTaskStruct { Heading : DirectionDegreesFloat32 } — degrees.
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", e.fixedRecord(e.directionDegreesEl(headingDegrees)));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("ChangeHeading task {} -> {} ({} deg true)", taskId, entityUuid, headingDegrees);
return taskId;
}

send(handle, params);
logger.info("ChangeSpeed task {} -> {} ({} m/s)", taskId, entityUuid, speedMetersPerSecond);
return taskId;
}
