// ── Base parameters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* Parameters common to every {@code Task} subclass.
*
* <p>Only {@code Entity} and {@code TaskId} are Required. {@code UniqueId}
* and {@code SendTime} are formally Optional but NETN-ETR states they are
* "required for all ETR related interactions" — {@code UniqueId} in
* particular is what a {@code SMC_Response} correlates against, so omitting
* it costs the acknowledgement.
*
* <p>{@code StartTime} is deliberately omitted: its documented default is
* the current {@code SendTime}, i.e. "start now", which is what every REST
* endpoint means. Sending an explicit value would only add a rounding error.
*/
private ParameterHandleValueMap taskBase(InteractionClassHandle handle,
UUID taskId,
String entityUuid,
String annotation,
boolean concurrent) throws Exception {
ParameterHandleValueMap params = newParams(8);
put(params, handle, "Entity", enc().uuid(entityUuid));
put(params, handle, "TaskId", enc().uuid(taskId));
// UniqueId is deliberately the same value as TaskId. SMC_Response.Action
// references the *interaction's* UniqueId, not the TaskId, so a distinct
// random value here would make every response impossible to attribute to
// the task that caused it — which is the one thing we need it for. The
// value is still unique: a fresh TaskId is generated per request.
putOptional(params, handle, "UniqueId", enc().uuid(taskId));
putOptional(params, handle, "SendTime", enc().epochTime(null));
putOptional(params, handle, "TaskMode", enc().octet(
concurrent ? TaskEnums.TASK_MODE_CONCURRENT : TaskEnums.TASK_MODE_NON_CONCURRENT));
if (annotation != null && !annotation.isBlank()) {
putOptional(params, handle, "Annotation", enc().unicodeString(annotation));
}
putTasker(params, handle);
return params;
}
/**
* Parameters common to the entity-control actions that are not tasks —
* {@code CancelTasks}, {@code SetSuppliesStatus}, {@code Aggregate}. These
* have no TaskId, TaskMode or Annotation.
*/
private ParameterHandleValueMap controlBase(InteractionClassHandle handle,
String entityUuid,
UUID actionId) throws Exception {
ParameterHandleValueMap params = newParams(4);
put(params, handle, "Entity", enc().uuid(entityUuid));
putOptional(params, handle, "UniqueId", enc().uuid(actionId));
putOptional(params, handle, "SendTime", enc().epochTime(null));
return params;
}
/**
* {@code Tasker} is a UUID in v4. Omitted unless a real entity UUID is
* configured: the FOM's default for a missing Tasker is "commander
* undefined", which is true, whereas a fabricated UUID would claim a
* commanding entity that does not exist in the federation.
*/
private void putTasker(ParameterHandleValueMap params, InteractionClassHandle handle) {
String taskerUuid = identity.taskerUuid();
if (taskerUuid == null || taskerUuid.isBlank()) {
return;
}
try {
putOptional(params, handle, "Tasker", enc().uuid(taskerUuid));
} catch (Exception e) {
logger.warn("TASKER_UUID '{}' is not a UUID, omitting Tasker: {}",
taskerUuid, e.getMessage());
}
}
// ── Movement ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** {@code Task.MoveToLocation}, TaskParameters = MoveToLocationTaskStruct. */
@Override
public UUID moveToLocation(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond,
String annotation) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(MOVE_TO_LOCATION);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params = taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid,
annotation == null || annotation.isBlank() ? "CSE bridge move order" : annotation,
false);
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", moveToLocationTaskParameters(
latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters, moveType, speedMetersPerSecond));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("MoveToLocation task {} -> {} ({}, {}) alt={}m speed={}m/s moveType={}",
taskId, entityUuid, latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters,
speedMetersPerSecond == null ? 0.0 : speedMetersPerSecond,
TaskEnums.moveType(moveType));
return taskId;
}
/**
* {@code MoveToLocationTaskStruct} = {Location, Path, MoveType, Speed}.
*
* <p>Extracted so {@link #describeMoveTask} previews the bytes this method
* actually sends, rather than a second implementation that might drift from
* it.
*
* <p>{@code Path} is sent empty: the destination is given, and NETN leaves
* route-finding to the simulator when no path is supplied. {@code Speed} 0
* means "the entity's own default" — note that a receiver taking 0 literally
* will hold the entity stationary, which is worth ruling out first when a
* move is accepted but nothing happens.
*/
private byte[] moveToLocationTaskParameters(double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
HlaEncoder e = enc();
return e.fixedRecord(
e.locationEl(latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters),
e.locationArrayEl(List.of()),
e.int32El(TaskEnums.moveType(moveType)),
e.float32El(speedMetersPerSecond == null ? 0.0 : speedMetersPerSecond));
}
@Override
public Map<String, String> describeMoveTask(double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
HlaEncoder e = enc();
double[] ecef = HlaEncoder.wgs84ToEcef(latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters);
double speed = speedMetersPerSecond == null ? 0.0 : speedMetersPerSecond;
byte[] taskParameters = moveToLocationTaskParameters(
latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters, moveType, speedMetersPerSecond);
Map<String, String> fields = new LinkedHashMap<>();
fields.put("interactionClass", MOVE_TO_LOCATION);
fields.put("TaskParameters.datatype", "MoveToLocationTaskStruct (HLAfixedRecord)");
fields.put("TaskParameters.Location.datatype",
"LocationStruct = 3 x MeterFloat64 {X, Y, Z}, GEOCENTRIC ECEF METRES "
+ "(IEEE 1278.1-2012 section 1.6.3 world coordinate system) — NOT degrees");
fields.put("TaskParameters.Location.input",
String.format("latitude %.6f, longitude %.6f, altitude %.1f m (WGS-84)",
latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters));
fields.put("TaskParameters.Location.encodedAs",
String.format("X=%.3f Y=%.3f Z=%.3f (metres)", ecef[0], ecef[1], ecef[2]));
fields.put("TaskParameters.Location.hex",
HlaEncoder.hex(e.fixedRecord(e.locationEl(latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters))));
fields.put("TaskParameters.Path",
"LocationStructArray, sent EMPTY (element count 0) — route-finding left to the "
+ "receiving simulator");
fields.put("TaskParameters.MoveType",
"MoveTypeEnum32 = " + TaskEnums.moveType(moveType)
+ " (0 CrossCountry, 1 OnlyRoads, 2 RoadsAndCrossCountry) from moveType='"
+ moveType + "'");
fields.put("TaskParameters.Speed",
String.format("VelocityMeterPerSecondFloat32 = %.3f m/s%s", speed,
speed == 0.0 ? " — 0 means 'entity default'; a receiver reading it "
+ "literally would keep the entity stationary" : ""));
fields.put("TaskParameters.hex", HlaEncoder.hex(taskParameters));
fields.put("TaskParameters.lengthBytes", String.valueOf(taskParameters.length));
fields.put("inheritedParameters",
"Entity (UUID, 16 raw bytes), TaskId (UUID), UniqueId (= TaskId, what "
+ "SMC_Response.Action references), SendTime (EpochTime int32 seconds), "
+ "TaskMode (TaskModeEnum8 octet), Annotation (HLAunicodeString)"
+ (identity.taskerUuid() == null || identity.taskerUuid().isBlank()
? ", Tasker omitted (TASKER_UUID unset)" : ", Tasker (UUID)"));
return fields;
}
/**
* {@code Task.Patrol}, TaskParameters = PatrolTaskStruct.
*
* <p>v4 merged {@code PatrolRepeating} into {@code Patrol}: the repeat is
* expressed by the {@code Duration} and {@code IntervalTime} fields rather
* than by a different class.
*/
@Override
public UUID patrol(String entityUuid,
List<double[]> waypointsLatLonAlt,
String patrolType,
String patrolMoveType,
Integer durationSeconds,
Integer intervalSeconds,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(PATROL);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge patrol order", false);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
// {Path (ArrayOfWaypoints), PatrolType, MoveType, Duration, IntervalTime}
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", e.fixedRecord(
e.waypointArrayEl(waypointsLatLonAlt,
speedMetersPerSecond == null ? 0.0 : speedMetersPerSecond, 0.0),
e.int32El(TaskEnums.patrolType(patrolType)),
e.int32El(TaskEnums.patrolMoveType(patrolMoveType)),
e.millisEl(TaskEnums.secondsToMillis(durationSeconds)),
e.millisEl(TaskEnums.secondsToMillis(intervalSeconds))));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("Patrol task {} -> {} ({} waypoints, duration {}s, interval {}s)",
taskId, entityUuid, waypointsLatLonAlt.size(), durationSeconds, intervalSeconds);
return taskId;
}
/** {@code Task.MagicMove}, TaskParameters = MagicMoveTaskStruct{Location, Heading}. */
@Override
public UUID magicMove(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
double headingDegrees) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(MAGIC_MOVE);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge magic move", false);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
// Heading is DirectionDegreesFloat32 — degrees, unlike v2's radians.
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", e.fixedRecord(
e.locationEl(latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters),
e.directionDegreesEl(headingDegrees)));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("MagicMove task {} -> {} ({}, {}) heading {}deg",
taskId, entityUuid, latitude, longitude, TaskEnums.normaliseDegrees(headingDegrees));
return taskId;
}
// ── Fires ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* {@code Task.IndirectFire} (v2 FireAtLocation), TaskParameters =
* IndirectFireTaskStruct{Area, Duration, Round}.
*
* <p>{@code Area} is a {@code LocationStructArray}. The REST call gives a
* single point, which is sent as a one-element area — the target point
* itself. Pass a polygon here if the simulator rejects a degenerate area.
*/
@Override
public UUID fireAtLocation(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
int durationSeconds) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(INDIRECT_FIRE);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge fire mission", false);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", e.fixedRecord(
e.locationArrayEl(List.of(new double[]{latitude, longitude, altitudeMeters})),
e.millisEl(TaskEnums.secondsToMillis(Math.max(0, durationSeconds))),
unspecifiedRound(e)));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("IndirectFire task {} -> {} ({}, {}) for {}s",
taskId, entityUuid, latitude, longitude, durationSeconds);
return taskId;
}
/** {@code Task.DirectFire} (v2 FireAtEntity), TaskParameters = DirectFireTaskStruct. */
@Override
public UUID fireAtEntity(String entityUuid, String targetUuid) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(DIRECT_FIRE);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge engagement order", false);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
// {Target, Duration, Round}. Duration 0 = "as required", per the FOM.
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", e.fixedRecord(
e.uuidEl(targetUuid),
e.millisEl(0L),
unspecifiedRound(e)));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("DirectFire task {} -> {} at target {}", taskId, entityUuid, targetUuid);
return taskId;
}
/**
* A {@code RoundStruct} that specifies nothing.
*
* <p>v4 requires weapon and munition types on every fire task, but DIS
* enumerations are scenario-specific: guessing one produces a
* plausible-looking engagement with the wrong weapon. All-zero types with
* zero quantity and rate mean "simulator decides", which is both honest and
* the closest equivalent to v2, where these fields did not exist.
*/
private hla.rti1516e.encoding.DataElement unspecifiedRound(HlaEncoder e) {
return e.roundEl(TaskEnums.TYPE_UNSPECIFIED, TaskEnums.TYPE_UNSPECIFIED, 0, 0.0);
}
// ── Control ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** {@code Task.ChangeRulesOfEngagement} (v2 SetRulesOfEngagement). */
@Override
public UUID setRulesOfEngagement(String entityUuid, String roe) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(CHANGE_ROE);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge ROE change", true);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters",
e.fixedRecord(e.octetEl(TaskEnums.rulesOfEngagement(roe))));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("ChangeRulesOfEngagement task {} -> {} ({})", taskId, entityUuid, roe);
return taskId;
}
/**
* {@code CancelTasks} with an empty {@code Tasks} array.
*
* <p>v4 merged CancelAllTasks and CancelSpecifiedTasks: the FOM defines an
* empty or absent list as "cancel all scheduled and ongoing tasks".
*/
@Override
public UUID cancelAllTasks(String entityUuid) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(CANCEL_TASKS);
UUID actionId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params = controlBase(handle, entityUuid, actionId);
put(params, handle, "Tasks", enc().arrayOfUuid(List.of()));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("CancelTasks {} -> {} (all tasks)", actionId, entityUuid);
return actionId;
}
/**
* {@code SetSuppliesStatus} (v2 MagicResource), from NETN-LOG.
*
* <p>Note the semantic change: MagicResource added resources, whereas
* SetSuppliesStatus <em>sets</em> the new quantity of one supply type. The
* FOM also restricts it to {@code AggregateEntity}, so applying it to a
* platform or lifeform may be refused by the simulator.
*/
@Override
public UUID injectSupplies(String entityUuid, String resourceType, String supplyType,
double quantity) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(SET_SUPPLIES_STATUS);
UUID actionId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params = controlBase(handle, entityUuid, actionId);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
put(params, handle, "SupplyStatus", e.fixedRecord(
e.entityTypeEl(TaskEnums.supplyType(resourceType, supplyType)),
e.float64El(quantity)));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("SetSuppliesStatus {} -> {} ({} = {}). Note v4 sets the quantity rather than "
+ "adding to it, and the FOM restricts this action to aggregate entities.",
actionId, entityUuid, resourceType, quantity);
return actionId;
}
// ── Environment ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* {@code Task.CreateObstacle} from NETN-SE, TaskParameters =
* CreateObstacleTaskStruct{EngineeringTask{EngineeringObjectId, Name,
* Duration}, Area}.
*
* <p>The obstacle type is no longer part of the task: NETN-SE models the
* result as an {@code EnvironmentObject} whose type the engineering federate
* decides, so a supplied {@code obstacleType} is only logged.
*/
@Override
public UUID createObstacle(String entityUuid,
String obstacleName,
String obstacleType,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double radiusMeters) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(CREATE_OBSTACLE);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
UUID obstacleUuid = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge obstacle: " + obstacleName, false);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", e.fixedRecord(
e.recordEl(
e.uuidEl(obstacleUuid),
e.stringEl(obstacleName == null ? "obstacle" : obstacleName),
e.millisEl(0L)),           // Duration 0 = simulator's own estimate
e.locationArrayEl(HlaEncoder.circleToPolygon(
latitude, longitude, radiusMeters, 0.0, AREA_POLYGON_POINTS))));
send(handle, params);
if (obstacleType != null && !obstacleType.isBlank()) {
logger.info("obstacleType '{}' is not sent in v4 — NETN-SE lets the engineering "
+ "federate choose the resulting EnvironmentObject type.", obstacleType);
}
logger.info("CreateObstacle task {} -> {} obstacle {} ({}, {}) r={}m as a {}-point area",
taskId, entityUuid, obstacleUuid, latitude, longitude, radiusMeters,
AREA_POLYGON_POINTS);
return obstacleUuid;
}
/**
* {@code Task.DisruptCommunication} from NETN-COM, TaskParameters =
* {Network (UUID), Area (LocationStructArray), DisruptionEnabled}.
*
* <p>v4 identifies the network by the UUID of a NETN-COM
* {@code CommunicationNetwork} object rather than by name. A name cannot be
* resolved to a UUID without subscribing to those objects, so a non-UUID
* {@code network} is rejected — the all-zero UUID means "all networks",
* which is what omitting it should mean, and silently widening a request
* from one network to all of them would be worse than an error.
*/
@Override
public UUID disruptCommunication(String entityUuid,
String network,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double radiusKm,
boolean enabled) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(DISRUPT_COMMUNICATION);
UUID taskId = UUID.randomUUID();
ParameterHandleValueMap params =
taskBase(handle, taskId, entityUuid, "CSE bridge comms disruption", true);
HlaEncoder e = enc();
hla.rti1516e.encoding.DataElement networkEl;
if (network == null || network.isBlank()) {
networkEl = e.nilUuidEl();
} else {
try {
networkEl = e.uuidEl(network);
} catch (IllegalArgumentException bad) {
throw new IllegalArgumentException(
"NETN v4 identifies a communication network by UUID, not by name, so "
+ "\"network\": \"" + network + "\" cannot be used. Omit \"network\" to "
+ "disrupt all networks, or pass the UUID of the NETN-COM "
+ "CommunicationNetwork object.");
}
}
put(params, handle, "TaskParameters", e.fixedRecord(
networkEl,
e.locationArrayEl(HlaEncoder.circleToPolygon(
latitude, longitude, radiusKm * 1000.0, 0.0, AREA_POLYGON_POINTS)),
e.booleanEl(enabled)));
send(handle, params);
logger.info("DisruptCommunication task {} -> {} {} around ({}, {}) r={}km",
taskId, entityUuid, enabled ? "enabled" : "disabled", latitude, longitude, radiusKm);
return taskId;
}
// ── Multi-resolution modelling ────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* {@code Aggregate} / {@code Disaggregate} from NETN-MRM 3.0.
*
* <p>Two v2 concepts disappear here. The request is no longer addressed to a
* federate — it goes to the entity, and the RTI routes it to whichever
* federate has modelling responsibility — so {@code federate} and
* {@code MRM_FEDERATE} are unused. And {@code RemoveSubunits} is gone:
* aggregation in v4 always deletes the entities listed in
* {@code DisaggregatedEntities}.
*/
@Override
public UUID multiResolution(boolean aggregate,
String aggregateUuid,
String federateName,
boolean removeSubunits) throws Exception {
InteractionClassHandle handle = interaction(aggregate ? MRM_AGGREGATE : MRM_DISAGGREGATE);
UUID actionId = UUID.randomUUID();
// Aggregate has no parameters of its own; Disaggregate has an optional
// Formation, omitted so the simulator uses its default formation.
ParameterHandleValueMap params = controlBase(handle, aggregateUuid, actionId);
send(handle, params);
if (federateName != null && !federateName.isBlank()) {
logger.info("Ignoring \"federate\": \"{}\" — v4 MRM addresses the aggregate entity, "
+ "and the RTI routes the action to whichever federate owns it.", federateName);
}
if (aggregate && removeSubunits) {
logger.info("Ignoring \"removeSubunits\" — v4 aggregation always deletes the "
+ "disaggregated entities.");
}
logger.info("MRM {} {} entity {}",
aggregate ? "Aggregate" : "Disaggregate", actionId, aggregateUuid);
return actionId;
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/TaskDialect.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
/**
* One NETN tasking dialect.
*
* <h2>Why an interface</h2>
* NETN restructured tasking between FOM versions and the two schemes are
* mutually unintelligible:
*
* <ul>
*   <li><b>v2-era</b> ({@link EtrV2Dialect}) — {@code ETR_Root.ETR_Task.*} with
*       flat parameters, {@code Taskee}, {@code StartWhen} as a Datetime18
*       string, angles in radians. This is what {@code SWORD_FOM.xml} defines,
*       so it remains the dialect for any federation SWORD created.</li>
*   <li><b>v4</b> ({@link SmcV4Dialect}) — {@code SMC_EntityControl.Task.*} with
*       one {@code TaskParameters} struct per task, {@code Entity},
*       {@code StartTime} as EpochTime, angles in degrees.</li>
* </ul>
*
* <p>Which one applies is not ours to choose: the bridge may join a federation
* created by another federate, and the FOM that federation was created with
* decides. Sending the wrong dialect is not an error the RTI reports — the
* class simply does not exist, or worse, exists with different parameters — so
* the dialect is detected at connect time from the FOM actually loaded (see
* {@code BridgeFederate.selectDialect}) and both implementations are kept.
*
* <p>Methods take and return the REST API's vocabulary (degrees, seconds,
* lat/lon, human-readable enum names) so that the HTTP contract is identical
* whichever dialect is live. Each implementation converts to what its FOM wants.
*
* <p>All eleven operations below are implemented by both dialects, but not
* always identically, and never approximately:
* <ul>
*   <li>A parameter one dialect has no field for is ignored with a log line —
*       {@code speedMetersPerSecond} on v2, {@code federate} and
*       {@code removeSubunits} on v4.</li>
*   <li>A value one dialect cannot represent is rejected with
*       {@link IllegalArgumentException} (HTTP 400) — a communication network
*       given by name on v4, which identifies networks by UUID.</li>
*   <li>{@link UnsupportedTaskException} (HTTP 409) is for when no dialect is
*       available at all, and for any future operation a dialect genuinely
*       cannot express.</li>
* </ul>
*/
public interface TaskDialect {
/**
* The REST operations a dialect implements.
*
* <p>Exists so that "which FOM class does /move send?" has one answer per
* dialect, in one place, usable by the REST response, the diagnostics
* endpoints and the task log alike. The alternative — repeating the v2/v4
* name pair at each call site — is how the two get out of step.
*/
enum Op {
MOVE, PATROL, MAGIC_MOVE, FIRE_AT_LOCATION, FIRE_AT_ENTITY, ROE, CANCEL_TASKS,
INJECT_SUPPLIES, CREATE_OBSTACLE, DISRUPT_COMMS, AGGREGATE, DISAGGREGATE,
CHANGE_HEADING, CHANGE_SPEED, CHANGE_ALTITUDE, STOP
}
/** The FOM class name this dialect sends for an operation. */
String classFor(Op op);
/**
* Exactly what {@code /move} would put on the wire, without sending it.
*
* <p>Built for reconciling with another federate's decoder. When two teams
* implement {@code MoveToLocationTaskStruct} independently from the same
* spec, the failure mode is not an error — it is both sides being confident
* and one of them reading ECEF metres as degrees. Comparing field values and
* hex settles it in one message instead of several days.
*
* <p>Implementations must build the value through the same code path used to
* send it: a preview that agrees with the spec but not with the sender would
* be worse than none.
*
* @return ordered field name to description, including the encoded bytes
*/
Map<String, String> describeMoveTask(double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception;
/** Short identifier used in configuration and logs: {@code v2} or {@code v4}. */
String id();
/** Human-readable name, e.g. "NETN v2-era (ETR_Root.ETR_Task)". */
String name();
/**
* The interaction class whose presence in the loaded FOM proves this
* dialect is the one the federation speaks.
*/
String detectionClass();
/** Classes this dialect sends, and therefore must publish. */
List<String> published();
/** Classes this dialect listens for. */
List<String> subscribed();
/**
* Classes resolved only so the startup log records whether the FOM offers
* them. Not published — several live in optional modules (NETN-SE,
* NETN-COM, NETN-LOG) whose absence is normal and only matters to the
* endpoints that need them.
*/
List<String> optional();
// ── Movement ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
* @param annotation free text recorded with the task. Blank uses a default.
*        NETN treats this as a human-readable note, but a receiving federate
*        may parse it: the CSE-Prep drone mockup reads {@code range=} and
*        {@code alt=} key/value pairs from it to set an orbit radius, because
*        NETN-ETR has no orbit-radius field. See BridgeServer for how the
*        REST layer composes it.
*/
UUID moveToLocation(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
String moveType,
Double speedMetersPerSecond,
String annotation) throws Exception;
UUID patrol(String entityUuid,
List<double[]> waypointsLatLonAlt,
String patrolType,
String patrolMoveType,
Integer durationSeconds,
Integer intervalSeconds,
Double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception;
UUID magicMove(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
double headingDegrees) throws Exception;
// ── Fires ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
UUID fireAtLocation(String entityUuid,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double altitudeMeters,
int durationSeconds) throws Exception;
UUID fireAtEntity(String entityUuid, String targetUuid) throws Exception;
// ── Control ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
UUID setRulesOfEngagement(String entityUuid, String roe) throws Exception;
/**
* Turn to a heading, in <em>degrees</em> clockwise from true north.
*
* <p>The REST vocabulary is degrees in both dialects, but the wire is not:
* v4 {@code ChangeHeading} carries DirectionDegreesFloat32 while v2-era
* {@code TurnToHeading} carries AngleRadianFloat32. Each implementation
* converts; callers never see the difference.
*/
UUID changeHeading(String entityUuid, double headingDegrees) throws Exception;
/** Set the ordered speed in metres per second. */
UUID changeSpeed(String entityUuid, double speedMetersPerSecond) throws Exception;
/** Set the ordered altitude in metres above mean sea level. */
UUID changeAltitude(String entityUuid, double altitudeMeters) throws Exception;
/** Stop and hold. Carries no task parameters in either dialect. */
UUID stopAtSideOfRoad(String entityUuid) throws Exception;
UUID cancelAllTasks(String entityUuid) throws Exception;
UUID injectSupplies(String entityUuid,
String resourceType,
String supplyType,
double quantity) throws Exception;
// ── Environment ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** @return the UUID assigned to the created obstacle object */
UUID createObstacle(String entityUuid,
String obstacleName,
String obstacleType,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double radiusMeters) throws Exception;
UUID disruptCommunication(String entityUuid,
String network,
double latitude,
double longitude,
double radiusKm,
boolean enabled) throws Exception;
// ── Multi-resolution modelling ────────────────────────────────────────────
UUID multiResolution(boolean aggregate,
String aggregateUuid,
String federateName,
boolean removeSubunits) throws Exception;
/**
* Identity this bridge tasks under, and MRM defaults.
*
* @param taskerCallsign v2 {@code Tasker} is a Callsign string
* @param taskerUuid     v4 {@code Tasker} is a UUID reference to the
*                       commanding entity. Blank means omit the parameter,
*                       which the FOM defines as "commander undefined" —
*                       preferable to inventing a UUID that resolves to no
*                       entity in the federation.
* @param defaultMrmFederate v2 MRM requests must name the federate with
*                       modelling responsibility; v4 addresses the entity
*                       instead and ignores this.
*/
record Identity(String taskerCallsign, String taskerUuid, String defaultMrmFederate) {}
/**
* The live dialect cannot express this request.
*
* <p>Extends {@link IllegalStateException} so {@code BridgeServer.failure}
* maps it to HTTP 409 alongside "class not in the loaded FOM": from a
* caller's point of view both mean "this federation cannot do that", and
* the message says which.
*/
class UnsupportedTaskException extends IllegalStateException {
public UnsupportedTaskException(String message) {
super(message);
}
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/TaskEnums.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
/**
* The REST API's human-friendly vocabulary mapped onto FOM enumerations.
*
* <h2>Why this is shared between dialects</h2>
* NETN v4 restructured the tasking class tree and every task's parameters, but
* it did <em>not</em> renumber these enumerations: {@code MoveTypeEnum32},
* {@code PatrolTypeEnum32}, {@code PatrolMoveTypeEnum32},
* {@code RulesOfEngagementEnum8} and {@code TaskModeEnum8} have identical
* enumerators and values in NETN-ETR 2.0 and 3.0. Keeping one copy means a
* corrected value cannot end up applied to one dialect and not the other.
*
* <p>Legacy vocabulary used by existing callers is kept working rather than
* silently mapping to a wrong enumerator.
*/
final class TaskEnums {
private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(TaskEnums.class);
// MoveTypeEnum32
static final int MOVE_CROSS_COUNTRY           = 0;
static final int MOVE_ONLY_ROADS              = 1;
static final int MOVE_ROADS_AND_CROSS_COUNTRY = 2;
// PatrolTypeEnum32
static final int PATROL_OTHER   = 0;
static final int PATROL_CIRCLE  = 1;
static final int PATROL_REVERSE = 2;
// PatrolMoveTypeEnum32
static final int PATROL_MOVE_OTHER   = 0;
static final int PATROL_MOVE_STEALTH = 1;
static final int PATROL_MOVE_CAUTION = 2;
static final int PATROL_MOVE_OPEN    = 3;
// RulesOfEngagementEnum8
static final int ROE_HOLD_FIRE            = 0;
static final int ROE_FIRE_AT_WILL         = 1;
static final int ROE_FIRE_WHEN_FIRED_UPON = 2;
// TaskModeEnum8
static final int TASK_MODE_NON_CONCURRENT = 0;
static final int TASK_MODE_CONCURRENT     = 1;
// StopFreezeReasonEnum8 (RPR, dialect independent)
static final int STOP_REASON_RECESS      = 1;
static final int STOP_REASON_TERMINATION = 2;
/**
* Placeholder SISO-REF-010 types. DIS enumerations are simulator- and
* scenario-specific, so these are only sensible defaults: every endpoint
* accepts an explicit dotted type to override them, and a warning is logged
* whenever a default is used so it is obvious in the logs when a value
* needs confirming against the simulator's supported types.
*/
static final String SUPPLY_AMMUNITION = "6.1.0.1.0.0.0";
static final String SUPPLY_FUEL       = "6.1.0.2.0.0.0";
static final String SUPPLY_PROVISIONS = "6.1.0.3.0.0.0";
static final String SUPPLY_PERSONNEL  = "6.1.0.4.0.0.0";
static final String OBSTACLE_GENERIC  = "5.1.0.1.0.0.0";
/** All-zero EntityTypeStruct — "unspecified, simulator decides". */
static final String TYPE_UNSPECIFIED  = "0.0.0.0.0.0.0";
private TaskEnums() {}
static int moveType(String value) {
if (value == null) return MOVE_ROADS_AND_CROSS_COUNTRY;
return switch (value.trim().toLowerCase()) {
case "roads", "only_roads", "road"              -> MOVE_ONLY_ROADS;
case "cross_country", "crosscountry", "offroad" -> MOVE_CROSS_COUNTRY;
default                                         -> MOVE_ROADS_AND_CROSS_COUNTRY;
};
}
static int patrolType(String value) {
if (value == null) return PATROL_CIRCLE;
return switch (value.trim().toLowerCase()) {
case "loop", "circle", "circuit"             -> PATROL_CIRCLE;
case "pingpong", "reverse", "there_and_back" -> PATROL_REVERSE;
case "once", "other"                         -> PATROL_OTHER;
default                                      -> PATROL_CIRCLE;
};
}
static int patrolMoveType(String value) {
if (value == null) return PATROL_MOVE_OPEN;
return switch (value.trim().toLowerCase()) {
case "stealth", "covert"  -> PATROL_MOVE_STEALTH;
case "caution", "careful" -> PATROL_MOVE_CAUTION;
case "other"              -> PATROL_MOVE_OTHER;
default                   -> PATROL_MOVE_OPEN;
};
}
static int rulesOfEngagement(String value) {
if (value == null) return ROE_FIRE_WHEN_FIRED_UPON;
return switch (value.trim().toLowerCase()) {
case "hold_fire", "holdfire", "weapons_hold"      -> ROE_HOLD_FIRE;
case "fire_at_will", "fireatwill", "weapons_free" -> ROE_FIRE_AT_WILL;
default                                           -> ROE_FIRE_WHEN_FIRED_UPON;
};
}
static String supplyType(String resourceType, String explicitDisType) {
if (explicitDisType != null && !explicitDisType.isBlank()) {
return explicitDisType;
}
String key = resourceType == null ? "" : resourceType.trim().toLowerCase();
String mapped = switch (key) {
case "ammo", "ammunition", "munition" -> SUPPLY_AMMUNITION;
case "fuel", "petrol", "diesel"       -> SUPPLY_FUEL;
case "food", "provisions", "water"    -> SUPPLY_PROVISIONS;
case "personnel", "troops"            -> SUPPLY_PERSONNEL;
default                               -> SUPPLY_AMMUNITION;
};
logger.warn("Using placeholder DIS supply type {} for resourceType '{}'. Confirm the "
+ "correct SISO-REF-010 enumeration for this scenario and pass it as "
+ "\"supplyType\" to avoid relying on this default.", mapped, resourceType);
return mapped;
}
static String obstacleType(String name, String explicitDisType) {
if (explicitDisType != null && !explicitDisType.isBlank()) {
return explicitDisType;
}
logger.warn("Using placeholder DIS obstacle type {} for '{}'. Confirm the correct "
+ "SISO-REF-010 land cultural feature enumeration and pass it as "
+ "\"obstacleType\".", OBSTACLE_GENERIC, name);
return OBSTACLE_GENERIC;
}
static double normaliseDegrees(double degrees) {
double d = degrees % 360.0;
return d < 0 ? d + 360.0 : d;
}
/** Seconds (the REST vocabulary) to milliseconds (the v4 FOM vocabulary). */
static long secondsToMillis(Integer seconds) {
return seconds == null ? 0L : Math.max(0L, seconds.longValue() * 1000L);
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/model/Entity.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge.model;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
/**
* Represents a live entity in the HLA federation.
* Populated from NETN_Aggregate (Sword), NETN_GroundVehicle,
* NETN_Human, NETN_Aircraft (VBS4/SimWorks).
*/
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public class Entity {
// ── Identity ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
public String id;           // HLA object instance handle (string)
public String uniqueId;     // NETN UUID
public String name;         // Marking / AggregateMarking
public String callsign;     // NETN Callsign
public String symbolId;     // NETN SymbolId (SIDC)
// ── DIS identity (RPR EntityIdentifier) ───────────────────────────────
//
// The other way to name an entity, and the only way for a simulator that
// publishes no NETN UniqueId — which is the expected case for both MACE and
// VBS4. Unlike `id`, which is an RTI object instance handle and is unique
// only within one federation execution, this triple is the simulation's own
// identifier and is what a SetData datum has to be addressed to.
public Integer disSiteId;
public Integer disApplicationId;
public Integer disEntityNumber;
/** The triple formatted as "site-app-entity", or null if not yet reflected. */
public String entityIdentifier;
// ── Classification ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
public String entityType;   // "vehicle" | "human" | "aircraft" | "aggregate"
/**
* The FOM class this instance was discovered at, verbatim.
*
* <p>Reported because {@code entityType} is a bucket and buckets lose the one
* fact that matters when attributes are missing: an object is discovered at the
* most specific class <em>we subscribed to</em>, and only attributes available
* at that class can ever reach us. "entity" covers both
* {@code HLAobjectRoot.BaseEntity} and {@code …BaseEntity.PhysicalEntity}, which
* have very different attribute sets — so the bucket cannot distinguish "we are
* subscribed too shallowly" from "the publisher sent nothing". The class name
* can. Empty means the discovered class was one we never registered.
*/
public String discoveredClass;
public String simulator;    // "vbs4" | "sword" | "simworks" | "unknown"
public String dis;          // DIS entity type e.g. "1.1.222.4.3.1.0"
public String forceId;      // "friendly" | "opposing" | "neutral"
public String echelon;      // NETN Echelon (aggregates only)
// ── Position ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
public Double latitude;
public Double longitude;
public Double altitudeMeters;
public Double headingDegrees;
public Double speedMps;
// ── State ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
public String damageState;      // "no_damage" | "slight" | "moderate" | "destroyed"
public String aggregateState;   // "aggregated" | "disaggregated" (Sword only)
public String posture;          // NETN Posture (Sword only)
public String mission;          // NETN Mission (Sword only)
public String activity;         // NETN Activity
public Integer combatValue;     // 0-100 (Sword only)
public Boolean isCommandPost;   // NETN IsPC (Sword only)
// ── Timestamps ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
public Long discoveredAt;   // System.currentTimeMillis() when first seen
public Long lastUpdated;    // System.currentTimeMillis() of last update
public Entity() {}
public Entity(String id, String name, String entityType) {
this(id, name, entityType, null);
}
public Entity(String id, String name, String entityType, String discoveredClass) {
this.id = id;
this.name = name;
this.entityType = entityType;
this.discoveredClass = discoveredClass;
this.discoveredAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
this.lastUpdated  = System.currentTimeMillis();
}
public void touch() {
this.lastUpdated = System.currentTimeMillis();
}
}

[main/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/model/request/MoveRequest.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge.model.request;
/**
* Body of {@code POST /entities/{id}/move} — sent as ETR_Task.MoveToLocation
* (v2-era) or SMC_EntityControl.Task.MoveToLocation (v4).
*
* @param moveType        MoveTypeEnum32: "roads" | "cross_country" |
*                        "roads_and_cross_country" (default). For an air
*                        platform use "cross_country" — road-following is
*                        meaningless and a receiver that honours it may refuse.
* @param altitudeMeters  optional; defaults to the entity's last known
*                        altitude, or 0 if unknown. Folded into the geocentric
*                        Location, so for an orbiting drone this is the orbit
*                        altitude and it does matter.
* @param speedMetersPerSecond optional initial speed. Only NETN v4 carries a
*                        Speed field on this task, where it is required and the
*                        bridge sends 0 ("entity default") when omitted.
*                        Ignored on a v2-era federation, which expects a
*                        separate SetOrderedSpeed task.
* @param annotation      optional free text recorded with the task. NETN treats
*                        it as a human-readable note, but a receiver may parse
*                        it — see {@code orbitRadiusMeters}.
* @param orbitRadiusMeters optional. NETN-ETR has no orbit-radius field, so the
*                        CSE-Prep drone mockup reads {@code range=<metres>} out
*                        of the Annotation text instead. Setting this appends
*                        that key/value to the annotation. It is a convention of
*                        that federate, not part of the standard: a receiver
*                        that does not look for it simply sees a note.
*/
public record MoveRequest(
double latitude,
double longitude,
String moveType,
Double altitudeMeters,
Double speedMetersPerSecond,
String annotation,
Double orbitRadiusMeters
) {}

*                        ignored (with a log line) there.

public record PatrolRequest(

Double speedMetersPerSecond
) {

[test/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/AttributeRefreshTest.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import hla.rti1516e.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import java.io.Serializable;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.*;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;
/**
* The periodic attribute refresh must ask only about what is still missing.
*
* <p>These assertions are the only thing standing between a correct refresh and
* the failure it was written to fix: the FedPro client defaults to
* {@code asyncUpdates=false}, so every {@code requestAttributeValueUpdate} is a
* blocking round trip to the CRC — 0.7s to 8.5s each on CSE-Prep. Asking about
* all 116 discovered objects every 5 seconds saturates the LRC, and a saturated
* LRC stops answering the CRC's heartbeat and gets the federate ejected. None of
* that produces an error the bridge can see, which is why it is asserted here.
*/
class AttributeRefreshTest {
/** A plain Set that satisfies the RTI's AttributeHandleSet interface. */
static class TestAttributeSet extends HashSet<AttributeHandle>
implements AttributeHandleSet, Serializable {
@Override public AttributeHandleSet clone() {
TestAttributeSet copy = new TestAttributeSet();
copy.addAll(this);
return copy;
}
}
/** Likewise for the reflection payload; only the key set matters here. */
static class TestValueMap extends LinkedHashMap<AttributeHandle, byte[]>
implements AttributeHandleValueMap, Serializable {
@Override public hla.rti1516e.encoding.ByteWrapper getValueReference(AttributeHandle h) {
return null;
}
@Override public hla.rti1516e.encoding.ByteWrapper getValueReference(
AttributeHandle h, hla.rti1516e.encoding.ByteWrapper w) {
return null;
}
}
private RTIambassador rti;
private FederationAmbassador ambassador;
private ObjectClassHandle aircraft;
private AttributeHandle spatial, marking, entityType;
private final List<ObjectInstanceHandle> asked = new ArrayList<>();
@BeforeEach
void setUp() throws Exception {
rti        = mock(RTIambassador.class);
aircraft   = mock(ObjectClassHandle.class);
spatial    = mock(AttributeHandle.class);
marking    = mock(AttributeHandle.class);
entityType = mock(AttributeHandle.class);
AttributeHandleSetFactory factory = mock(AttributeHandleSetFactory.class);
when(factory.create()).thenAnswer(i -> new TestAttributeSet());
when(rti.getAttributeHandleSetFactory()).thenReturn(factory);
asked.clear();
doAnswer(invocation -> {
asked.add(invocation.getArgument(0));
return null;
}).when(rti).requestAttributeValueUpdate(
any(ObjectInstanceHandle.class), any(AttributeHandleSet.class), any());
ambassador = new FederationAmbassador(rti, new EntityStateStore());
TestAttributeSet subscribed = new TestAttributeSet();
subscribed.add(spatial);
subscribed.add(marking);
subscribed.add(entityType);
ambassador.registerSubscribedAttributes(aircraft, subscribed);
}
private ObjectInstanceHandle discover(String name) throws Exception {
ObjectInstanceHandle instance = mock(ObjectInstanceHandle.class);
when(instance.toString()).thenReturn(name);
ambassador.discoverObjectInstance(instance, aircraft, name);
return instance;
}
/**
* Discard the request discovery itself issues, so a test can assert about the
* periodic refresh alone. Discovery asking once is deliberate and is pinned by
* {@link #discoveryAsksOnceOnItsOwn()}; it is only noise for the other tests.
*/
private void ignoreDiscoveryRequests() {
asked.clear();
clearInvocations(rti);
}
private void reflect(ObjectInstanceHandle instance, AttributeHandle... attributes)
throws Exception {
TestValueMap values = new TestValueMap();
for (AttributeHandle attribute : attributes) {
values.put(attribute, new byte[] {0});
}
ambassador.reflectAttributeValues(instance, values, null, OrderType.RECEIVE,
mock(TransportationTypeHandle.class), null);
}
@Test
void discoveryAsksOnceOnItsOwn() throws Exception {
ObjectInstanceHandle instance = discover("HLA1");
assertEquals(List.of(instance), asked,
"discovery must ask immediately; waiting a full cycle is what left entities "
+ "as names with no position");
}
@Test
void asksForEverythingWhenNothingHasArrivedYet() throws Exception {
discover("HLA1");
ignoreDiscoveryRequests();
FederationAmbassador.RefreshSummary summary = ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(0);
assertEquals(1, summary.asked(), "a silent instance is the case the refresh exists for");
assertEquals(1, summary.outstanding());
assertEquals(0, summary.complete());
}
@Test
void stopsAskingOnceEveryAttributeHasArrived() throws Exception {
ObjectInstanceHandle instance = discover("HLA1");
reflect(instance, spatial, marking, entityType);
ignoreDiscoveryRequests();
FederationAmbassador.RefreshSummary summary = ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(0);
assertEquals(0, summary.asked(),
"a fully reported instance must cost nothing on every subsequent cycle");
assertEquals(1, summary.complete());
assertTrue(asked.isEmpty());
}
@Test
void keepsAskingWhilePartiallyReported() throws Exception {
ObjectInstanceHandle instance = discover("HLA1");
reflect(instance, spatial);   // Marking and EntityType still missing
ignoreDiscoveryRequests();
FederationAmbassador.RefreshSummary summary = ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(0);
assertEquals(1, summary.asked());
assertEquals(0, summary.complete());
}
@Test
void asksOnlyForTheMissingAttributes() throws Exception {
ObjectInstanceHandle instance = discover("HLA1");
reflect(instance, spatial);
ignoreDiscoveryRequests();
ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(0);
var captor = org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AttributeHandleSet.class);
verify(rti).requestAttributeValueUpdate(eq(instance), captor.capture(), any());
AttributeHandleSet requested = captor.getValue();
assertFalse(requested.contains(spatial), "already reported, so re-asking is pure cost");
assertTrue(requested.contains(marking));
assertTrue(requested.contains(entityType));
}
@Test
void capBoundsOneCycleAndRotatesSoNothingStarves() throws Exception {
List<ObjectInstanceHandle> fleet = new ArrayList<>();
for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
fleet.add(discover("HLA" + i));
}
ignoreDiscoveryRequests();
FederationAmbassador.RefreshSummary first = ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(4);
assertEquals(4, first.asked(), "the cap is what keeps a backlog from saturating the LRC");
assertEquals(10, first.outstanding());
assertTrue(first.capped());
List<ObjectInstanceHandle> firstPass = new ArrayList<>(asked);
asked.clear();
ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(4);
List<ObjectInstanceHandle> secondPass = new ArrayList<>(asked);
assertEquals(4, secondPass.size());
assertTrue(java.util.Collections.disjoint(firstPass, secondPass),
"a cap that re-asked the same head every cycle would never reach the tail");
}
@Test
void forgetsInstancesThatAreRemoved() throws Exception {
ObjectInstanceHandle instance = discover("HLA1");
reflect(instance, spatial, marking, entityType);
ignoreDiscoveryRequests();
assertEquals(1, ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(0).complete());
ambassador.removeObjectInstance(instance, null, OrderType.RECEIVE, null);
FederationAmbassador.RefreshSummary summary = ambassador.requestValuesForAllInstances(0);
assertEquals(0, summary.complete());
assertEquals(0, summary.outstanding());
}
}

[test/java/com/decho/cse/bridge/HlaEncoderTest.java]
package com.decho.cse.bridge;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
/**
* Encoding regression tests.
*
* <p>These assert the exact wire sizes and bytes the FOM requires. They caught
* nothing at the time of writing — the point is that a future change to
* {@link HlaEncoder} that silently alters a wire format shows up here instead
* of as a task the simulator quietly refuses.
*
* <p>No RTI connection is needed: {@code RtiFactoryFactory.getRtiFactory()} is
* a ServiceLoader lookup and the encoder factory it returns is pure
* client-side code.
*/
class HlaEncoderTest {
private final HlaEncoder encoder;
HlaEncoderTest() throws Exception {
encoder = new HlaEncoder();
}
@Test
void uuidIsSixteenRawBytesNotAString() {
byte[] encoded = encoder.uuid("3f2504e0-4f89-11d3-9a0c-0305e82c3301");
assertEquals(16, encoded.length, "UUID is an HLAfixedArray of 16 HLAbyte");
assertArrayEquals(new byte[]{
(byte) 0x3f, (byte) 0x25, (byte) 0x04, (byte) 0xe0,
(byte) 0x4f, (byte) 0x89, (byte) 0x11, (byte) 0xd3,
(byte) 0x9a, (byte) 0x0c, (byte) 0x03, (byte) 0x05,
(byte) 0xe8, (byte) 0x2c, (byte) 0x33, (byte) 0x01},
encoded);
// Dashes are optional.
assertArrayEquals(encoded, encoder.uuid("3f2504e04f8911d39a0c0305e82c3301"));
}
@Test
void nonUuidIdentifiersAreRejected() {
// An HLA object-instance handle string must never be sent as a Taskee.
assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
() -> HlaEncoder.parseUuid("HLAobjectInstanceHandle<42>"));
assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> HlaEncoder.parseUuid(""));
}
@Test
void worldLocationIsGeocentricEcefMetres() {
byte[] encoded = encoder.worldLocation(51.5, -0.12, 35);
assertEquals(24, encoded.length, "WorldLocationStruct is 3 x HLAfloat64BE");
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded); // big endian by default
double x = buffer.getDouble();
double y = buffer.getDouble();
double z = buffer.getDouble();
double radius = Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z);
assertTrue(radius > 6_350_000 && radius < 6_380_000,
"Should be an Earth-radius ECEF vector, was " + radius);
// Sanity: not latitude/longitude degrees, which is what was sent before.
assertTrue(Math.abs(x) > 1000, "X must be metres, not degrees");
}
@Test
void ecefConversionMatchesKnownPoints() {
double[] equator = HlaEncoder.wgs84ToEcef(0, 0, 0);
assertEquals(6378137.0, equator[0], 0.5);
assertEquals(0.0, equator[1], 0.5);
assertEquals(0.0, equator[2], 0.5);
double[] pole = HlaEncoder.wgs84ToEcef(90, 0, 0);
assertEquals(0.0, pole[0], 0.5);
assertEquals(0.0, pole[1], 0.5);
assertEquals(6356752.3, pole[2], 0.5);
}
@Test
void datetime18IsEighteenUtf16Characters() {
byte[] immediate = encoder.datetime18(null);
assertEquals(36, immediate.length, "18 HLAunicodeChar = 36 bytes UTF-16BE");
assertEquals(HlaEncoder.IMMEDIATE, new String(immediate, StandardCharsets.UTF_16BE));
byte[] stamped = encoder.datetime18(Instant.parse("2026-08-13T12:34:56.789Z"));
assertEquals(36, stamped.length);
assertEquals("20260813123456.789", new String(stamped, StandardCharsets.UTF_16BE));
}
@Test
void scalarWidthsMatchTheFomRepresentations() {
assertEquals(1, encoder.octet(2).length, "*Enum8 is HLAoctet");
assertEquals(4, encoder.int32(2).length, "*Enum32 / TimeSecInt32 is HLAinteger32BE");
assertEquals(4, encoder.float32(1.57).length, "AngleRadianFloat32");
assertEquals(8, encoder.float64(1.57).length, "MeterFloat64");
// HLAboolean and RPRboolean are different widths and not interchangeable.
assertEquals(4, encoder.hlaBoolean(true).length, "HLAboolean is HLAinteger32BE");
assertEquals(1, encoder.rprBoolean(true).length, "RPRboolean is HLAoctet");
// HLAunicodeString: 4-byte count + UTF-16BE characters.
assertEquals(4 + 6, encoder.unicodeString("CSE").length);
}
@Test
void arraysCarryTheirElementCount() {
byte[] path = encoder.arrayOfWorldLocation(List.of(
new double[]{51.5, -0.12, 0},
new double[]{51.6, -0.13, 0},
new double[]{51.7, -0.14, 0}));
// 4-byte count, padded to the 8-byte element boundary, then 3 x 24.
assertEquals(4 + 4 + 3 * 24, path.length);
assertEquals(3, ByteBuffer.wrap(path).getInt());
byte[] ids = encoder.arrayOfUuid(List.of(UUID.randomUUID(), UUID.randomUUID()));
assertEquals(4 + 32, ids.length, "UUID elements are octet aligned, so no padding");
assertEquals(2, ByteBuffer.wrap(ids).getInt());
}
@Test
void disStructuresHaveTheirSpecifiedLayout() {
byte[] entityType = encoder.entityType("6.1.0.2.0.0.0");
assertEquals(8, entityType.length, "EntityTypeStruct");
assertEquals(6, entityType[0], "EntityKind");
assertEquals(1, entityType[1], "Domain");
assertEquals(2, entityType[4], "Category");
assertEquals(12, encoder.supply("6.1.0.2.0.0.0", 500).length,
"NETN_SupplyStruct is EntityTypeStruct + float32");
assertEquals(6, encoder.entityIdentifier(1, 1, 0).length, "EntityIdentifierStruct");
assertEquals(8, encoder.clockTime(Instant.now()).length, "ClockTimeStruct");
}
@Test
void geodeticShapesUseDegreesNotEcef() {
byte[] quad = encoder.geodeticQuadrangle(51.4, -0.2, 51.6, 0.0);
assertEquals(32, quad.length, "GeodeticQuadrangle is 2 x GeodeticLocation");
assertEquals(51.4, ByteBuffer.wrap(quad).getDouble(), 1e-9,
"GeodeticLocation is plain degrees, unlike WorldLocationStruct");
}
// ══ NETN v4 ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// These matter more than the v2 ones above: the v4 encodings have never been
// exercised against a live RTI, and every difference from v2 is a wrong-value
// trap rather than a decode error — degrees vs radians, milliseconds vs
// seconds, one struct vs flat parameters. A failure here is the cheapest
// possible place to find out.
@Test
void epochTimeIsFourByteSecondsNotADatetimeString() {
Instant when = Instant.parse("2026-08-13T12:34:56Z");
byte[] encoded = encoder.epochTime(when);
assertEquals(4, encoded.length, "EpochTime is HLAinteger32BE seconds");
assertEquals(when.getEpochSecond(), ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded).getInt(),
"v4 replaced the 36-byte Datetime18 string with epoch seconds");
}
@Test
void uuidNestedInARecordMatchesTheRawSixteenBytes() {
// uuidEl() goes through the RTI's HLAfixedArray of HLAbyte, while uuid()
// writes the 16 raw bytes directly. They must agree, or every v4 task
// that references an entity inside a TaskParameters struct is malformed.
UUID id = UUID.fromString("3f2504e0-4f89-11d3-9a0c-0305e82c3301");
assertArrayEquals(encoder.uuid(id), encoder.fixedRecord(encoder.uuidEl(id)),
"UUID is octet aligned with no count prefix, nested or not");
}
@Test
void magicMoveTaskStructIsEcefLocationThenHeadingInDegrees() {
// MagicMoveTaskStruct{Location (LocationStruct), Heading
// (DirectionDegreesFloat32)}: 3 x float64 = 24, then a 4-aligned float32
// at offset 24, no trailing padding.
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(
encoder.locationEl(51.5, -0.12, 35),
encoder.directionDegreesEl(-90));
assertEquals(24 + 4, encoded.length);
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded);
double x = buffer.getDouble();
double y = buffer.getDouble();
double z = buffer.getDouble();
double radius = Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z);
assertTrue(radius > 6_350_000 && radius < 6_380_000,
"v4 LocationStruct is geocentric ECEF metres, not lat/lon degrees. Was " + radius);
assertEquals(270.0f, buffer.getFloat(), 1e-6,
"DirectionDegreesFloat32 is degrees in [0,360), unlike v2's radians");
}
@Test
void moveToLocationTaskStructKeepsFomFieldOrder() {
// {Location, Path (LocationStructArray), MoveType (int32), Speed (float32)}
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(
encoder.locationEl(51.5, -0.12, 0),
encoder.locationArrayEl(List.of()),
encoder.int32El(1),      // MoveTypeEnum32.OnlyRoads
encoder.float32El(12.5));
// Assert the two ends of the struct rather than its total length: what
// an empty variable array contributes in padding is the encoder's
// business, but the destination must lead and the speed must trail.
double[] expectedEcef = HlaEncoder.wgs84ToEcef(51.5, -0.12, 0);
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded);
assertEquals(expectedEcef[0], buffer.getDouble(), 1e-6, "Location.X leads the struct");
assertEquals(expectedEcef[1], buffer.getDouble(), 1e-6, "Location.Y");
assertEquals(expectedEcef[2], buffer.getDouble(), 1e-6, "Location.Z");
assertEquals(12.5f, buffer.getFloat(encoded.length - 4), 1e-6,
"Speed is the last field");
}
@Test
void patrolWaypointsCarrySpeedAndWidthPerPoint() {
byte[] path = encoder.fixedRecord(encoder.waypointArrayEl(List.of(
new double[]{51.5, -0.12, 0},
new double[]{51.6, -0.13, 0}), 8.0, 0.0));
assertEquals(2, ByteBuffer.wrap(path).getInt(), "element count leads the array");
// Waypoint{LocationStruct (24), Speed (4), SegmentMaxWidth (4)} = 32,
// 8-aligned, so the 4-byte count is padded to 8 first.
assertEquals(4 + 4 + 2 * 32, path.length);
}
@Test
void durationsAreMillisecondsAsInt64() {
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(encoder.millisEl(90_000L));
assertEquals(8, encoded.length, "TimeMillisecondInt64 is HLAinteger64BE");
assertEquals(90_000L, ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded).getLong(),
"v4 durations are milliseconds; v2 used int32 seconds");
assertEquals(90_000L, TaskEnums.secondsToMillis(90),
"the REST API keeps taking seconds");
}
@Test
void changeRulesOfEngagementTaskStructIsASingleOctet() {
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(encoder.octetEl(TaskEnums.ROE_HOLD_FIRE));
assertEquals(1, encoded.length, "RulesOfEngagementEnum8 is an HLAoctet");
assertEquals(0, encoded[0]);
}
@Test
void hlaBooleanInAStructIsFourBytesNotOne() {
// DisruptCommunicationTaskStruct.DisruptionEnabled is HLAboolean. Sending
// the 1-byte RPRboolean here would misalign everything after it.
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(encoder.booleanEl(true));
assertEquals(4, encoded.length);
assertEquals(1, ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded).getInt());
}
@Test
void roundStructIsTwoEntityTypesThenCountAndRate() {
// RoundStruct{WeaponType (8), MunitionType (8), QuantityFired (int16),
// RateOfFire (float32)}: the float32 is 4-aligned, so there are 2 bytes
// of padding after the count.
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(
encoder.roundEl(TaskEnums.TYPE_UNSPECIFIED, TaskEnums.TYPE_UNSPECIFIED, 0, 0.0));
assertEquals(8 + 8 + 2 + 2 + 4, encoded.length);
for (byte b : encoded) {
assertEquals(0, b, "an unspecified round must be all zeroes");
}
}
@Test
void cancelTasksSendsAnEmptyButPresentArray() {
// v4 merged CancelAllTasks into CancelTasks, where an empty Tasks array
// means "all". The array still has to be sent: Tasks is Required.
byte[] encoded = encoder.arrayOfUuid(List.of());
assertEquals(4, encoded.length);
assertEquals(0, ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded).getInt());
}
@Test
void circleToPolygonStaysWithinRadiusAndDoesNotRepeatTheFirstPoint() {
List<double[]> polygon = HlaEncoder.circleToPolygon(51.5, -0.12, 500, 0, 8);
assertEquals(8, polygon.size());
assertFalse(polygon.get(0)[0] == polygon.get(7)[0]
&& polygon.get(0)[1] == polygon.get(7)[1],
"the closing vertex is implied, not repeated");
for (double[] point : polygon) {
// ~500 m is well under 0.01 degrees of latitude.
assertEquals(51.5, point[0], 0.01);
assertEquals(-0.12, point[1], 0.02);
}
}
// ── Published wire contract ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// These pin the golden vectors in WIRE_CONTRACT.md, which is handed to other
// federates as the thing to implement against. If our encoder changes, either
// the document is updated with it or one of these fails — a published contract
// that quietly stops matching the sender is worse than no document.
//
// Reference point: latitude 60.476, longitude 25.116, altitude 500 m.
// Metre values are compared with a tolerance because cos/sin may differ by
// 1 ulp between platforms; lengths and offsets are exact.
private static final double VECTOR_LAT = 60.476;
private static final double VECTOR_LON = 25.116;
private static final double VECTOR_ALT = 500.0;
private static final double VECTOR_X = 2853356.7941;
private static final double VECTOR_Y = 1337581.7704;
private static final double VECTOR_Z = 5527238.2030;
@Test
void vectorA_locationStructMatchesThePublishedContract() {
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(
encoder.locationEl(VECTOR_LAT, VECTOR_LON, VECTOR_ALT));
assertEquals(24, encoded.length, "LocationStruct is 3 x MeterFloat64, no padding");
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded);
assertEquals(VECTOR_X, buffer.getDouble(), 1e-4, "WIRE_CONTRACT.md vector A: X");
assertEquals(VECTOR_Y, buffer.getDouble(), 1e-4, "WIRE_CONTRACT.md vector A: Y");
assertEquals(VECTOR_Z, buffer.getDouble(), 1e-4, "WIRE_CONTRACT.md vector A: Z");
}
@Test
void vectorB_magicMoveTaskStructMatchesThePublishedContract() {
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(
encoder.locationEl(VECTOR_LAT, VECTOR_LON, VECTOR_ALT),
encoder.directionDegreesEl(270.0));
assertEquals(28, encoded.length, "WIRE_CONTRACT.md vector B is 28 bytes");
assertEquals(270.0f, ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded).getFloat(24), 1e-6,
"Heading is degrees at offsets 24..27");
}
@Test
void vectorD_populatedArrayCarriesTheAlignmentPadding() {
// The padding a receiver must not forget: count, then 4 bytes so the first
// element starts 8-aligned. A decoder that omits it is 4 bytes out for the
// whole array, which is the defect found in one peer implementation.
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(encoder.locationArrayEl(List.of(
new double[]{60.476, 25.116, 0},
new double[]{60.500, 25.200, 0},
new double[]{60.550, 25.300, 0})));
assertEquals(4 + 4 + 3 * 24, encoded.length, "WIRE_CONTRACT.md vector D is 80 bytes");
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded);
assertEquals(3, buffer.getInt(0), "element count at offsets 0..3");
assertEquals(0, buffer.getInt(4), "padding at offsets 4..7");
// element[0].X begins at offset 8, and is an Earth-radius ECEF component.
assertTrue(Math.abs(buffer.getDouble(8)) > 1000,
"element[0].X begins at offset 8 and is in metres");
}
@Test
void vectorC_emptyPathLengthIsWhicheverTheRtiEncoderChooses() {
// The one case IEEE 1516's wording leaves ambiguous: whether an empty
// variable array carries the padding that would precede a first element.
// Both 36 and 40 are defensible readings, so this asserts the invariants
// that hold either way and records which one this RTI implements.
//
// It matters because a receiver assuming 36 while we send 40 reads Speed
// from MoveType's bytes — zero — and a receiver honouring Speed = 0
// literally will hold the entity still. Accepted, unmoved, nothing logged.
byte[] encoded = encoder.fixedRecord(
encoder.locationEl(VECTOR_LAT, VECTOR_LON, VECTOR_ALT),
encoder.locationArrayEl(List.of()),
encoder.int32El(0),
encoder.float32El(30.0));
assertTrue(encoded.length == 36 || encoded.length == 40,
"MoveToLocationTaskStruct with an empty Path should be 36 or 40 bytes, was "
+ encoded.length + ". WIRE_CONTRACT.md section C explains both.");
// Speed is always the final four bytes, whichever reading applies.
assertEquals(30.0f, ByteBuffer.wrap(encoded).getFloat(encoded.length - 4), 1e-6,
"Speed is the last field");
System.out.println("WIRE_CONTRACT.md vector C: this RTI encodes an empty "
+ "LocationStructArray such that MoveToLocationTaskStruct is "
+ encoded.length + " bytes (Speed at offset " + (encoded.length - 4)
+ "). Update WIRE_CONTRACT.md section C to state this form only.");
}
@Test
void areaVariantEncodesDiscriminantThenPaddedAlternative() {
byte[] circle = encoder.areaCircle(51.5, -0.12, 50);
// 4-byte GeoLocationTypeEnum32 discriminant, padded to the alternative's
// 8-byte boundary, then GeodeticLocation (16) + MeterFloat32 (4).
// The RTI encoder adds no trailing padding.
assertEquals(4 + 4 + 16 + 4, circle.length);
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(circle);
assertEquals(2, buffer.getInt(), "GeoLocationTypeEnum32.Circle = 2");
assertEquals(0, buffer.getInt(), "alignment padding");
assertEquals(51.5, buffer.getDouble(), 1e-9);
assertEquals(-0.12, buffer.getDouble(), 1e-9);
assertEquals(50.0f, buffer.getFloat(), 1e-6);
}
}
